TL;DR: 该工作提出局部凸松弛层级与高效可并行消息传递算法，用于估计量子多体系统基态能量下界，并给出可证效率与实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 凸松弛层级可在多项式时间内给出量子多体系统基态能量下界，但传统求解器计算开销大、效率保证稀缺，难以扩展到大规模系统和精确近似。

Method: 构建局部松弛层级，并设计基于次梯度法与Nesterov型加速梯度下降（作用于熵平滑目标）的两种消息传递算法；层级形式包括基于成对约化密度矩阵局部一致性的层级，以及基于一致区间的层级。

Result: 证明第一层级在树上的对易哈密顿量上精确；一致区间层级对链上可分离哈密顿量弱扰动以区间尺寸指数收敛；在有界度图上，两种消息传递算法分别以O(n/ε²)与O(n/ε)时间运行（假设最优消息范数为O(1)，实验中观察到该条件成立）；在不同哈密顿量、晶格几何与松弛层级上完成基准测试。

Conclusion: 局部松弛层级配合消息传递算法为大规模量子多体基态能量估计提供了高效、可并行且具理论保证的经典方法，实验验证理论预测并显示其有望超越标准凸优化求解器；代码已开源。

Abstract: Convex relaxation hierarchies provide lower bounds to the ground state energy of quantum many-body systems that can be computed in polynomial time on a classical computer, at any fixed hierarchy level. However, scaling these methods to large systems and accurate approximations remains challenging due to the computational cost of traditional solvers and the scarcity of efficiency guarantees. In this work, we develop local relaxation hierarchies and efficient, highly parallelisable message passing algorithms for estimating the relaxed ground state energies. We show that the first level of the hierarchy---based on local consistency of pairwise reduced density matrices---is exact for commuting Hamiltonians on trees. We further establish that another hierarchy, based on consistent intervals, converges exponentially fast in the interval size to the ground state energy for weak perturbations of separable Hamiltonians on a chain, thereby providing an efficient classical algorithm for these systems. Then, we introduce two variants of message passing algorithms that run in $\mathcal{O}(n/ε^2)$ and $\mathcal{O}(n/ε)$ time for any fixed level of the local hierarchy on bounded-degree graphs, where $ε$ is the precision for the relaxed ground state energy per site. This assumes that the optimal messages have $\mathcal{O}(1)$ norm---a condition we observe in practical settings in our experiments. These algorithms are based on the subgradient method and the Nesterov-type accelerated gradient descent method applied to an entropy-smoothed objective. Finally, we benchmark the message passing algorithms across different quantum Hamiltonians, lattice geometries, and relaxation levels, validating the theoretical predictions and their potential to surpass standard convex optimisation solvers for this problem. We release the resulting library at github.com/rick1924/gse-message-passing.

</details>


### [237] [The Single-Copy Quantum Bandit Is Classical: An Exact Spectral Collapse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40339)
*Siu Hin Ng*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: We study multi-armed bandits whose arms supply unknown quantum states and whose mean rewards are defined by a known effect $F$. Each fresh copy may be measured by an arbitrary, adaptively chosen POVM. We prove an exact collapse: the measured relative entropy from an arm state to its confusing reward half-space equals the classical Burnetas-Katehakis functional of $F$'s spectral statistics, and the spectral measurement of $F$ together with an explicit least-favorable state, built from the derivative of the matrix logarithm, forms a saddle point of the underlying measurement game. Consequently, spectral measurement followed by classical KL-UCB is asymptotically instance-optimal among all consistent single-copy policies, in every finite dimension and for every reward effect under the stated nondegeneracy assumptions: adaptive and randomized measurement design cannot improve the leading logarithmic regret coefficient, and neither can storing copies for later single-copy measurement. A self-contained finite-time bound covers general effects and boundary distributions. Any further improvement must come from measurements outside the single-copy class. For consistent policies with arbitrary quantum memory, an amortized relative-entropy argument gives a converse with the Umegaki half-space divergence, and the two per-arm regret floors, $1/K_{\mathrm{inf}}$ and $1/D_{\mathrm{inf}}$, coincide if and only if the arm commutes with $F$. Whether the Umegaki floor is attainable involves a composite quantum Stein problem and a separate reduction to adaptive regret; we pose it as an open problem, with two-copy numerical evidence.

</details>


### [238] [Quantum de Finetti theorems for states and channels in any distance measure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40343)
*Liuhang Ye,Bjarne Bergh,Nilanjana Datta*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在算子不等式层面证明标准与Renner指数de Finetti定理，统一给出任意Schatten范数与满足数据处理不等式量子Rényi散度的界，并推广到信道de Finetti表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统有限量子de Finetti定理以迹距离给出iid混合近似，精度为k²/n量级，且不直接适用于信道层级；作者希望得到更强的算子序形式界，并回答Berta等人关于置换协变信道CP支配逼近的问题。

Method: 采用算子不等式（而非迹距离）刻画置换不变态的k-系统边缘与iid混合之间的序关系；利用Choi态算子序等价于信道CP序这一性质，将态层结论提升到信道层；对置换协变信道N⁽ⁿ⁾:A^{⊗n}→B^{⊗n}研究k-系统约化信道的CP支配；在无信号条件下构造Choi-几乎iid混合并处理r个缺陷的指数逼近。

Result: 标准情形下固定局域维度时max-相对熵误差界由k²/n改进为k/n；信道情形下k-系统约化信道被张量幂信道混合CP支配，误差O(k/√n)且对局域维度呈多项式依赖；无信号条件下证明指数信道de Finetti定理，r个缺陷时误差上界为poly(n)(2d_A⁴k³/(nr²))^{(r+1)/2}，对合适参数随n指数衰减。

Conclusion: 算子不等式方法同时强化了标准与Renner de Finetti定理，给出更优的误差标度并自然衔接信道CP序，解决了Berta等人提出的置换协变信道逼近问题，为量子信息与优化中的信道表示提供新工具。

Abstract: Standard finite quantum de Finetti theorems approximate the $k$-system marginals of permutation-invariant states of $n$-systems by mixtures of independent and identically distributed (iid) states, usually in trace distance. We prove both standard and Renner's exponential de Finetti theorems in the stronger form of operator inequalities, implying bounds in every Schatten norm and for every quantum Rényi divergence satisfying data processing. In the standard case, for fixed local dimension, our $k/n$ error bound in max-relative entropy improves on the previously best known $k^2/n$ scaling, even in the classical setting. The operator-inequality approach is particularly suited to study channel de Finetti representations because operator order between Choi states is equivalent to completely positive (CP) order between the underlying channels. For permutation-covariant channels $N^{(n)}:A^{\otimes n}\to B^{\otimes n}$, where $d_A=\dim A$, we prove that the $k$-system reduced channel is CP-dominated by a mixture of tensor-power channels with error $O(k/\sqrt{n})$ and polynomial dependence on the local dimensions, addressing a question raised by Berta et al. [Math. Program. 194, 781-829 (2022)]. Under the no-signalling condition, we also prove an exponential channel de Finetti theorem where the approximating mixture consists of Choi-almost-iid channels, whose normalized Choi states are almost-iid in the sense of Mazzola-Sutter-Renner. In the case of $r$ defects, the representation error is at most $\mathrm{poly}(n)\bigl(2d_A^4k^3/(nr^2)\bigr)^{(r+1)/2}$ and decays exponentially in $n$ for a suitable choice of parameters $r$ and $k$.

</details>


### [239] [Gibbs Sampling in the Shattered Phase by Decoded Quantum Interferometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40345)
*Leo Zhou,Noah Shutty,Mark Sellke,Stephen P. Jordan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 将解码量子干涉（DQI）应用于经典Ising自旋哈密顿量的Gibbs采样，证明该采样问题可归约为量子解码问题，且DQI可达温度由解码算法性能决定；在随机Erdős-Rényi超图上的Ising k-spin玻璃中，碎裂与无序混沌构成阻碍稳定算法的拓扑壁垒，而基于Prange方法的经典算法与配备量子解码器的DQI均可突破该壁垒，在远超动力学相变阈值的逆温度下完成采样。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典Ising自旋玻璃的Gibbs采样在低温区存在由碎裂（shattering）与无序混沌（disorder chaos）造成的动力学相变，形成拓扑壁垒，使Glauber动力学及任何输出分布对输入扰动"稳定"的算法失效。作者希望探究量子方法（特别是Decoded Quantum Interferometry, DQI）能否突破这一壁垒，并建立Gibbs采样与量子解码之间的理论联系。

Method: 1) 将经典Ising自旋哈密顿量的Gibbs采样问题归约为量子解码问题，明确DQI可达温度与解码算法性能的对应关系；2) 研究对象为随机Erdős-Rényi超图（平均度D≥k）上的经典Ising k-spin玻璃，等价于Max-k-XORSAT；3) 从预测的动力学相变β_dyn(k,D)=√(2ln k/D)×[1+o(1)]起始的温度区间内，分析碎裂与无序混沌形成的拓扑壁垒对Glauber动力学及稳定算法的阻碍；4) 分别用基于Prange方法的经典算法和配备量子解码器的DQI进行采样，证明壁垒可被突破。

Result: 在β_dyn起始的温度范围内，碎裂与无序混沌构成拓扑壁垒，阻碍包括Glauber动力学在内的多种算法，以及任何输出分布在输入扰动下"稳定"的算法。该壁垒可被基于Prange方法的经典算法和配备量子解码器的DQI共同突破。例如当D=αk（固定α>1）时，对于足够大的k，Prange算法与DQI均可在任意逆温度β<tanh⁻¹(1/α)下采样，远超动力学阈值β_dyn∼√(2ln k/(αk))。

Conclusion: DQI能够克服阻碍稳定算法的拓扑壁垒，表明量子解码能力可转化为超越经典稳定算法局限的Gibbs采样能力；同时，Gibbs采样与量子解码之间的归约为设计突破动力学相变限制的采样算法提供了新框架。

Abstract: We apply Decoded Quantum Interferometry (DQI) to sample from the Gibbs measures of classical Ising spin Hamiltonians. We show that this Gibbs sampling problem reduces to a quantum decoding problem, and the temperature achievable by DQI is determined by the performance of decoding algorithms. We then focus on the task of Gibbs sampling for classical Ising $k$-spin glasses (or Max-$k$-XORSAT) on random Erdős-Rényi hypergraphs with average degree $D\ge k$. In a temperature range beginning asymptotically at the predicted dynamical phase transition, $β_{\rm dyn}(k,D) = \sqrt{(2\ln k)/D}\times [1+o_{k\to\infty}(1)]$, we show that shattering and disorder chaos form a topological barrier that obstructs many algorithms, including Glauber dynamics and any algorithm whose output distribution is "stable" under perturbations of the input. In contrast, we prove that this barrier can be broken both by a classical algorithm based on Prange's method, and by DQI equipped with a quantum decoder. For example, when $D=αk$ with fixed $α>1$, both Prange's algorithm and DQI can sample at any inverse temperature $β< \tanh^{-1}(1/α)$ for sufficiently large $k$, well beyond the dynamical threshold $β_{\rm dyn} \sim \sqrt{2\ln k / (αk)}$. Therefore, our results show that DQI can overcome topological barriers that obstruct stable algorithms.

</details>


### [240] [Efficient Calculation of Equilibrium Correlation Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40349)
*Yizhi Shen,Roel Van Beeumen,Wibe A. de Jong,Efekan Kökcü*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出一种仅用单量子线路并行计算平衡关联函数的方法：以O(log N_t)辅助比特编码时间，总量子资源为O(N_t log N_t)，并证明该标度最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平衡关联函数G_AB(t-t')是物理系统可观测动力学量的核心描述。现有量子算法（Hadamard测试、变分法、线性响应类算法）对每个时间点都需独立线路，导致至少O(N_t)次采样与O(N_t^2)总量子门，时间点增多时开销急剧上升。

Method: 将时间作为量子变量存入O(log N_t)个辅助比特，用单一量子线路一次性计算所有时间点的平衡关联函数，把对时间点的串行重复替换为时间维度的量子并行；该线路仅需运行O(log N_t)次。

Result: 大系统尺寸极限下总量子资源为O(N_t log N_t)，证明该标度达到最优；并以一维无自旋Hubbard模型的格林函数计算作为演示验证。

Conclusion: 通过时间量子编码实现关联函数计算的并行化，将资源复杂度从O(N_t^2)降至O(N_t log N_t)且具最优性，为量子模拟动力学关联函数提供了更高效方案。

Abstract: A majority of measurable dynamic quantities of a physical system is described as an equilibrium correlation function of the form $G_{AB}(t-t') = \left<A(t)B(t')\right>$, where $t'$ represents the impact time and $t$ represents the response time. Conventional quantum algorithms to compute such quantities via Hamiltonian simulation such as the Hadamard test, variational methods, or linear-response-based algorithms share one feature in common: each time point $t-t'$ is calculated by a different quantum circuit, leading to at least $O(N_t)$ total shots, and at least $O(N_t^2)$ total quantum gates. Here, we provide a different approach: utilizing $O(\log N_t)$ ancilla qubits to store time as a quantum variable, and effectively parallelize the computation of an equilibrium correlation function by requiring only a single quantum circuit for all time points. We show that this circuit needs to be run only $O(\log N_t)$ times, which in total requires $O(N_t \log N_t)$ quantum resources in the large system size limits. We prove that this scaling is optimal, and demonstrate our algorithm calculating the Green's function of a one-dimensional spinless Hubbard model.

</details>


### [241] [On The Simplest Quantum-Secure Block Cipher](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40350)
*Gorjan Alagic,Joseph Carolan,Christian Majenz,Saliha Tokat*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明两轮Even-Mansour密码在理想置换模型（IPM）下对自适应量子敌手信息论安全，且证明任意仅调用一次公开置换的构造在量子情形下不安全，从而说明该构造具有最小性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 伪随机置换（PRP）在密码学中广泛使用，而抗量子查询的PRP日益重要。经典安全的Even-Mansour框架在量子查询下的安全性尚不明确：单轮已被基于Simon算法的量子攻击攻破，多轮仅在非自适应敌手模型下有安全性结果。因此需要确定两轮Even-Mansour在自适应量子敌手下的安全性。

Method: 采用压缩置换预言机（compressed permutation oracles）技术，并构造一个特殊设计的等距变换（isometry）来关联理想实验与真实实验，从而给出两轮Even-Mansour在IPM下对抗多项式次自适应正/逆量子查询敌手的信息论安全性证明；同时通过下界论证说明单次调用公开置换的构造在量子下不安全。

Result: 证明两轮Even-Mansour密码在IPM中对抗多项式次自适应正向与逆向量子查询的信息论安全性；并证明该构造在最小性意义下最优，即本质上任何仅通过一次公开置换调用构造的密码在量子情形下均不安全。

Conclusion: 两轮Even-Mansour是量子安全的密钥交替构造，且轮数为量子安全所需的最少轮数，明确了该框架在量子查询模型下的安全地位。

Abstract: Pseudorandom permutations are ubiquitous in theoretical and applied cryptography. PRPs that offer security even against adversaries making quantum queries are of increasing interest, and used in applications ranging from constructing pseudorandom unitaries to separating SZK from BQP.
  A successful framework for constructing classically-secure PRPs is the key-alternating Even-Mansour approach, which interleaves applications of public permutations with additions of round keys. The single-round construction is already classically secure in the ideal permutation model (IPM), with added rounds offering improved concrete security. However, in the quantum-query setting, the status of this framework is presently unclear. A simple quantum-query attack based on Simon's algorithm breaks the one-round cipher. For two or more rounds, security is only known against non-adaptive adversaries who must prepare all queries in advance.
  In this work, we show that the two-round Even-Mansour cipher is information theoretically secure in the IPM against adversaries making polynomially-many adaptive forward and inverse quantum queries to all available oracles. Our proof uses compressed permutation oracles and a specially crafted isometry relating the ideal and real experiments. We also show that this construction is minimal, in the sense that essentially any cipher constructed via a single call to a public permutation is quantumly insecure.

</details>


### [242] [All Unitaries Have Constant Depth Quantum Circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40351)
*Barak Nehoran,Henry Yuen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明所有 $n$ 比特酉算符都可用深度 $\poly(n,\log 1/\epsilon)$ 的单、双比特门电路近似实现（辅以 $2^{O(n)}$ 个辅助比特）；若允许无界扇出门，深度可进一步降至常数级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统认知中，一般 $n$ 比特酉算符需要 $2^{O(n)}$ 深度的量子电路实现，但指数深度是否为必要条件一直悬而未决。作者试图解决这一开放性问题，探究在允许大量辅助比特的前提下，一般酉算符是否可被并行化为多项式深度。

Method: 构造性地将任意 $n$ 比特酉算符以算子范数 $\epsilon$ 近似，使用单、双比特门构建深度为 $\poly(n,\log 1/\epsilon)$ 的电路，辅以 $2^{O(n)}$ 个辅助比特；进一步利用无界扇出门将深度压缩至常数。核心创新在于建立了 Aaronson-Kuperberg 酉合成问题与复杂性理论及密码学中的局部可解码码（LDCs）和私有信息检索（PIR）之间的新联系。

Result: 成功证明所有酉算符均可并行化为多项式深度电路，并可在允许无界扇出门时压缩为常数深度，从而否定了指数深度对一般酉算符的必要性。

Conclusion: 指数深度并非实现一般酉算符的必要条件；通过建立酉合成与 LDC/PIR 的跨领域联系，实现了酉算符的多项式乃至常数深度并行化，为该开放问题给出了出乎意料的否定性答案。

Abstract: It is well-known that every $n$-qubit unitary can be implemented by a $2^{O(n)}$-depth quantum circuit using single- and two-qubit gates. It has been open whether exponential depth is \emph{necessary} for general unitaries, even when allowing for unlimited number of ancilla qubits. We show, perhaps surprisingly, that all unitaries can be approximated to operator norm $ε$ by a circuit of one- and two-qubit gates of depth $\poly(n,\log 1/ε)$ with $2^{O(n)}$ ancilla qubits. In other words, every $n$-qubit unitary can be parallelized to polynomial depth. Moreover, if we allow unbounded fan-out gates, these circuits can be reduced further to \emph{constant} depth. Our construction takes advantage of a novel relationship connecting the unitary synthesis problem of Aaronson and Kuperberg to locally-decodable codes and private information retrieval from complexity theory and cryptography.

</details>


### [243] [Quantum hypothesis testing of non-mixed-unitarity: A multifaceted hierarchy of quantum channel discrimination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40355)
*Pratik Ghosal,Pritam Halder,Ayan Patra,Aditi Sen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 研究未知量子信道是否为混合酉信道，将问题建模为复合信道假设检验，刻画不同探测态与辅助存储限制下的Stein指数，并建立多层级严格分离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 判定未知量子信道属于指定的非混合酉信道还是混合酉信道集合，具有基础意义；需要在不同探测资源限制下刻画可达到的最优错误指数（Stein指数），并揭示资源限制带来的能力边界与分离现象。

Method: 将信道判别表述为复合信道假设检验问题；考虑并行策略，在逐步放宽探测态与辅助存储限制下刻画Stein指数；引入块i.i.d.探测（块内任意相关、块间独立），推导有限字母表达式；变化块大小及块内相关限制、辅助存储可用性，构建层级结构；利用钻石范数距离给出下界。

Result: 无辅助存储时，全i.i.d.探测对任意unital非混合酉信道Stein指数为零；三个乘积探测块即可使qutrit Werner-Holevo信道获得严格正指数；块大小为二时，乘积探测对O(3)-协变信道似乎不足，但纠缠探测可使qutrit Werner-Holevo信道获严格正指数，而所有最大纠缠探测均失败。有辅助存储时，全i.i.d.探测对任意非混合酉信道均足够，且无需输入-参考纠缠，指数可由到混合酉集合的钻石范数距离下界；对奇数维Werner-Holevo信道，该指数为无穷，且任意满Schmidt秩纯态均为最优。

Conclusion: 在复合信道假设检验框架下，探测态相关性、块结构与辅助存储共同构成Stein指数的多层级体系；辅助存储可显著提升判别能力并导致无穷指数，而无辅助存储时则出现零指数与严格正指数之间的尖锐分离。

Abstract: Given multiple uses of an unknown quantum channel, we determine whether it is a specified non-mixed-unitary channel or belongs to the set of mixed-unitary channels. Formulating this as a composite channel hypothesis testing problem, we characterize the Stein exponents achievable by parallel strategies under progressively weaker restrictions on probe states and auxiliary memory. In particular, we consider block-i.i.d. probes, which allow arbitrary correlations within blocks of fixed size while remaining i.i.d. across blocks, and derive finite-letter expressions of the corresponding Stein exponents. Varying the block size, which interpolates between fully i.i.d. and arbitrary probes, together with further restrictions on intra-block correlations and access to auxiliary memory, results in a multifaceted hierarchy. We establish several strict separations within this hierarchy. Without auxiliary memory, we prove that fully i.i.d. probes yield a vanishing Stein exponent for every unital non-mixed-unitary channel, while blocks of three product probes suffice to obtain a strictly positive exponent for the qutrit Werner-Holevo channel. For block size two, although product probes seem to be insufficient for O(3)-covariant channels, an entangled probe achieves a strictly positive exponent for the qutrit Werner-Holevo channel, even though every maximally entangled probe fails. In contrast, auxiliary memory makes fully i.i.d. probes sufficient by yielding a strictly positive exponent for every non-mixed-unitary channel, even without requiring input-reference entanglement, and this exponent admits a lower bound determined by the diamond-norm distance from the mixed-unitary set. Moreover, for odd-dimensional Werner-Holevo channels, we find that this exponent is infinite, with every full-Schmidt-rank pure state being optimal.

</details>


<div id='physics.app-ph'></div>

# physics.app-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [244] [Self-verifying time-resolved multicomponent gas analysis from raw sensor signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38663)
*YingCheng Zhou,Kosuke Minami,Genki Yoshikawa*

Main category: physics.app-ph

TL;DR: 提出一种物理约束神经网络，可同时估算时变气体浓度并重构传感器信号，实现单次测量的自验证多组分气体分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 气体传感器阵列可在实验室外估计混合气体组成，但由于真实浓度未知，通常无法判断估计结果是否可靠，缺乏自验证手段。

Method: 构建物理约束神经网络，同时输出时变浓度估计与对应传感器信号重构；以十二通道膜型表面应力传感器阵列（MSS）对浓度随时间变化的水-乙醇混合气体进行测试。

Result: 模型从原始信号重构出两组分的浓度曲线；通过实测与重构信号的不一致检测出单通道的人工偏移与灵敏度变化；通过违反吸附与力学方程检测出测量记录部分被删除或替换。

Conclusion: 该方法实现了单次测量下的时间分辨多组分气体分析与自验证，无需真实浓度标签即可评估结果可靠性。

Abstract: Gas sensor arrays can estimate the composition of gas mixtures from their response patterns. However, when a sensor is used outside the laboratory, the true gas concentrations are unknown, so there is usually no direct way to determine whether the estimated result is reliable. Here, we develop a physics-constrained neural network that estimates time-varying gas concentrations and reconstructs the corresponding sensor signals at the same time. We tested the method with a twelve-channel membrane-type surface stress sensor array exposed to water-ethanol mixtures whose concentrations changed over time. The model reconstructed the concentration profiles of both components from the raw signals. Artificial offsets and sensitivity changes in one channel were detected from disagreement between the measured and reconstructed signals. Deletion or replacement of part of a measurement record was detected from violations of the sorption and mechanical equations. This approach enables time-resolved multicomponent gas analysis with self-verification from a single measurement.

</details>


### [245] [Exchange-mediated directional coupling of short-wavelength spin waves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39249)
*Zhen Zhu,Roman Verba,Philipp Pirro,Andrii V. Chumak,Qi Wang*

Main category: physics.app-ph

TL;DR: 提出一种以磁性修饰YIG间隔层替代非磁性间隙的交换介导定向耦合器，在短波长（~100 nm）下实现约98%功率转移，器件尺寸仅400 nm × 30 nm。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 磁子电路小型化与提速需要更短波长的自旋波，但传统定向耦合器依赖空间分离波导间的动态偶极相互作用，在交换主导区迅速减弱，耦合长度随波长减小而增大，限制了器件进一步微型化。

Method: 用磁性修饰的YIG间隔层取代两波导间的非磁性间隙，构建交换介导的定向耦合器；建立解析模型描述该机制，并用微磁学模拟进行验证；据此设计无弯曲接入波导的直波导耦合器。

Result: 直耦合器占用面积仅400 nm × 30 nm；在自旋波波长约100 nm时，向目标波导转移约98%的归一化输出功率；间隔层阻尼大幅增加时性能几乎不变。

Conclusion: 该方案克服了偶极耦合器的短波长限制，为面向信息处理的高集成度磁子电路提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Miniaturizing magnonic circuits and increasing their processing speed require spin waves with progressively shorter wavelengths. However, conventional directional couplers rely on the dynamic dipolar interaction between spatially separated waveguides, which rapidly weakens in the exchange-dominated regime. As a result, their coupling length increases as the spin-wave wavelength decreases, limiting further device miniaturization. Here, we introduce an exchange-mediated directional coupler by replacing the nonmagnetic gap between two waveguides with a magnetically modified YIG spacer. The spacer provides a continuous exchange pathway and preserves the splitting between the symmetric and antisymmetric modes at large wavenumbers. We develop an analytical model to describe this mechanism and verify it using micromagnetic simulations. Based on this concept, we design a straight coupler without curved access waveguides, with a footprint of only 400 nm * 30 nm. For a spin-wave wavelength of approximately 100 nm, the device transfers approximately 98% of the normalized output power to the target waveguide. Its performance remains nearly unchanged even when the damping of the spacer is strongly increased. These results overcome the short-wavelength limitation of dipolar couplers and provide a route toward highly integrated magnonic circuits for information processing.

</details>


### [246] [Light-driven Modulation of 1-bit Metamaterial Unit Cells and Arrays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39516)
*Benjamin A Scott,Keiran J Cowan,Fraser Burton,Ken E Evans,Alexander W Powell*

Main category: physics.app-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种光热驱动的1-bit可重构反射阵单元，无需集成电源即可实现独立单元控制，并通过波导实验与4单元阵列验证了其在3.7GHz下的波束调控能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有形变材料天线阵列要么缺乏单元级独立控制，要么需要集成电源，导致波束扫描能力受限，并增加体积、重量与功耗。作者希望实现一种结构简单、可调、无需复杂电路与电源的通信阵列。

Method: 设计由二聚体元件构成的1-bit反射阵单元，通过光热激活的电桥实现单元间连接/断开以完成调制；采用形状记忆材料作为开关，并给出光热开关机制设计。通过波导实验验证单元性能，并构建4单元阵列，逐单元顺序激活进行测试。

Result: 单元在3.7GHz工作频率下的实验与仿真结果高度一致；4单元阵列中每个单元可被依次独立激活，仿真与实验吻合良好，证明可实现单元级独占控制。

Conclusion: 研究表明，1-bit单元可通过光热方式配置，无需复杂电子器件或集成电源，为新型形变反射阵天线开辟了路径。

Abstract: Shape-morphing materials have the potential to realise simple, tunable telecommunication arrays without the need for complex circuitry. However, existing devices either lack individual cell control, or require an integrated power supply, reducing their beamsteering ability and increasing size, weight and power consumption. This paper presents a simple, photothermally activated 1-bit reflectarray unit cell requiring no integrated power sources. The unit cell consists of dimer elements, which are connected or disconnected via a photothermally activated electrical bridge to achieve modulation. The paper discusses the shape memory materials used for switching, and presents a design for a photothermal switching mechanism. The unit cell is validated by waveguide experiments, showing strong agreement with simulations at the working frequency of 3.7GHz. Finally, a 4-cell array is created. Each cell is activated sequentially, with good agreement between simulation and experiment, demonstrating the ability to control each cell exclusively. This study therefore demonstrates that a 1-bit unit cell can be photothermally configured without complicated electronics or an integrated power supply, opening the door to a new class of shape-morphing reflectarray.

</details>


### [247] [Evaluating Advanced Impact Mitigation Technologies for Concussion Risk Reduction in Youth Cycling Helmets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39885)
*Jessica A Towns,Fady F Abayazid,Gerald A Grant,David B Camarillo,Nicholas J Cecchi*

Main category: physics.app-ph

TL;DR: 研究评估六种青少年自行车头盔技术在青少年特定冲击条件下的防护效果，发现先进技术可降低脑震荡风险，但效果取决于冲击条件与防护机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先进自行车头盔技术在青少年特定冲击条件下的有效性尚不明确，且头盔形变与头盔-头模相对旋转对防护的贡献机制需要澄清。

Method: 测试六种青少年自行车头盔技术：两种传统EPS对照（FOAM-A、FOAM-B）与四种先进技术（CELL、SLIP、SLIDE、HYDRAULIC）。以25°砧座2.9 m/s和45°砧座5.2 m/s在三个位置冲击佩戴头盔的头模，量化峰值线加速度（PLA）、峰值旋转加速度（PRA）及YGAMBIT预测的脑震荡风险；用立体高速摄像测量砧座法向头模位移（近似头盔形变）与头盔-头模相对旋转。

Result: 头盔技术显著影响两种条件下的预测脑震荡风险。2.9 m/s时，所有先进技术相对FOAM-A降低风险，但仅SLIDE与HYDRAULIC相对FOAM-B降低风险；5.2 m/s时所有先进技术相对两种泡沫对照均降低风险。HYDRAULIC在两种条件下风险最低，相对泡沫对照分别降低均值风险94–95%和77–78%。CELL、SLIP、SLIDE显著降低PRA，HYDRAULIC同时显著降低PLA与PRA。较大的砧座法向头模位移与较低PLA相关，较大相对旋转与较低PRA相关，二者独立贡献于预测脑震荡风险降低。

Conclusion: 青少年自行车头盔中的先进技术可降低青少年脑震荡风险，但有效性取决于冲击条件与缓解机制；最大化可控头盔形变与旋转解耦的设计可能在青少年相关冲击中提供更稳健的防护。

Abstract: The efficacy of advanced cycling helmet technologies under youth-specific impact conditions remains unclear. We evaluated how helmet technologies affect head kinematics and concussion risk and whether helmet deformation and helmet-headform relative rotation contribute to protection. Six youth bicycle helmet technologies were tested, including two conventional EPS controls (FOAM-A, FOAM-B) and four advanced technologies (CELL, SLIP, SLIDE, HYDRAULIC). Helmeted headforms were impacted at three locations using a 25 degree anvil at 2.9 m/s, and a 45 degree anvil at 5.2 m/s. Peak linear acceleration (PLA), peak rotational acceleration (PRA), and YGAMBIT-predicted concussion risk were quantified. Stereo high-speed videography measured anvil-normal headform displacement to approximate helmet deformation, and helmet-headform relative rotation. Helmet technology significantly affected predicted concussion risk under both conditions. At 2.9 m/s, all advanced technologies reduced risk relative to FOAM-A, while only SLIDE and HYDRAULIC reduced risk relative to FOAM-B. At 5.2 m/s, all advanced technologies reduced risk relative to both foam controls. HYDRAULIC produced the lowest risk in both conditions, reducing mean risk by 94-95% and 77-78% relative to the foam controls at 2.9 and 5.2 m/s, respectively. CELL, SLIP, and SLIDE significantly reduced PRA across both conditions, whereas HYDRAULIC significantly reduced both PLA and PRA. Greater anvil-normal headform displacement was associated with lower PLA, and greater relative rotation with lower PRA; both independently contributed to lower predicted concussion risk. Advanced technologies in youth cycling helmets can reduce youth concussion risk, but effectiveness depends on impact condition and mitigation mechanism. Designs maximizing controlled helmet deformation and rotational decoupling may provide robust protection in youth-relevant impacts.

</details>


### [248] [Bilayer Lithium Niobate Acoustic Resonators for Spurious-Free Wideband Operation at 6 GHz](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40044)
*Florian Hartmann,Silvan Stettler,Luis Guillermo Villanueva*

Main category: physics.app-ph

TL;DR: 提出双层X切铌酸锂厚度剪切体声波谐振器结构，利用对称性工程选择性激发二阶慢剪切模态并抑制杂散模式，在约6 GHz实现约35%的k²eff与18.6%的梯形滤波器分数带宽。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无线通信标准向更高频率与更宽带宽演进，对声学滤波器提出更高要求；传统谐振器在5 GHz以上受光刻精度限制、机电耦合系数（k²eff）下降及杂散模态激发等问题制约，难以实现实用化滤波器。

Method: 引入双层X切铌酸锂（LiNbO₃）厚度剪切体声波谐振器架构，以对称性工程作为新的设计自由度：将两片压电薄膜按定制键合角度垂直堆叠，从而选择性激发二阶厚度剪切慢模态（SH2, slow），并抑制X切LiNbO₃固有的快速厚度剪切模式，实现无杂散的带内响应。

Result: 约6 GHz工作的器件测得k²eff约为35%，与有限元仿真结果高度吻合，验证了对称性驱动的模态选择机制；概念验证梯形滤波器实现18.6%的分数带宽，展示了该架构的宽带能力。

Conclusion: 该构型可在保持高耦合系数与频谱纯度的同时实现由厚度定义的频率缩放，为下一代无线系统中的宽带射频滤波器提供了有前景的平台。

Abstract: The evolution of wireless communication standards toward higher frequencies and wider bandwidths places increasing demands on acoustic filter technologies. Conventional resonators face scaling limitations above 5 GHz, where lithographic constraints, reduced electromechanical coupling coefficient (k2eff), and spurious mode excitation hinder practical filter implementation. Here, we introduce a bilayer X-cut lithium niobate (LiNbO3) thickness-shear bulk acoustic resonator architecture that leverages symmetry engineering as a new design degree of freedom. By vertically stacking two piezoelectric thin films with a tailored bonding angle, the second-order thickness-shear mode (SH2, slow) is selectively excited. Furthermore, the fast thickness-shear modes inherent to X-cut LiNbO3 are suppressed, yielding a spurious-free in-band response. Devices operating around 6 GHz demonstrate a k2eff of approximately 35%, in close agreement with finite-element simulations and confirming the symmetry-driven mode selection mechanism. Proof-of-concept ladder filters demonstrate a fractional bandwidth of 18.6%, highlighting the wideband capabilities of the proposed architecture. The proposed configuration enables thickness-defined frequency scaling while maintaining high coupling and spectral purity, offering a promising platform for wideband radiofrequency filters in next-generation wireless systems.

</details>


<div id='physics.class-ph'></div>

# physics.class-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [249] [Automated Sailboat Navigation using Controllabilty-Aware Nonlinear Model Predicitive Control under Stochastic Winds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39320)
*Junzhuo Wu,Ya-Jun J Pan,Chao Shen,Sean Smith,Emmanuel Witrant*

Main category: physics.class-ph

TL;DR: 提出面向自动帆船随机风况的NMPC路径规划与控制框架，并用Lie代数分析识别纵荡一阶可控性丧失条件作为约束，仿真验证有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机风况下（阵风不可预测、风向突变、视风速波动）传统LoS与航点法依赖静态或确定性环境模型，导致规划与控制失效。

Method: 基于非线性模型预测控制（NMPC）设计路径规划器与控制器；对帆船动力学进行Lie代数分析，确定纵荡方向丧失一阶控制权限的工况，并将其作为约束嵌入NMPC；与基线规划/控制器对比。

Result: 仿真结果验证了所提框架的有效性，并能与基线方法形成对比。

Conclusion: 将可控性分析嵌入NMPC可在随机风扰动下实现时间高效的帆船轨迹规划与执行。

Abstract: This paper presents a systematic approach to plan and execute a time-efficient sailboat trajectory under the challenging stochastic wind conditions for automated sailboat. Unlike deterministic scenarios, stochastic wind disturbances introduce challenges such as gust unpredictability, directional shifts, and fluctuating apparent wind speeds. These characteristics render traditional planning methods, such as Line-of-Sight (LoS) and waypoint approaches, to be ineffective and not applicable, as they often rely on static or deterministic environmental models. This paper proposed a new path planner and controller based on nonlinear model predictive control method. It is compared with a baseline planner and controller. A Lie-algebraic analysis of the sailboat dynamics is carried out to identify the operating conditions under which the vessel loses first-order control authority in surge, and these conditions are embedded as constraints in the NMPC. Finally, simulation results are provided to validate the proposed framework.

</details>


<div id='cs.SD'></div>

# cs.SD [[Back]](#toc)

### [250] [When Does a Spoken Agent Have Enough Evidence to Act? The PACT-SLM Contract Test](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38232)
*Mengzhe Geng*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 该论文提出PACT-SLM评测协议，分别度量流式语音语言模型在部分语音下的动作身份与动作时机，并发现两者是决策行为的不同维度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流式口语智能体可能在语音证据尚不充分时提前执行外部动作，而仅看最终轮次得分无法判断每个已观测前缀是否真正支持该动作，因此需要将“动作身份”与“动作时机”分离评估。

Method: 提出Partial Speech Action Contract for Turn Taking in Speech Language Models（PACT-SLM），设定首个有效动作时间，分别测量动作身份与时机。主诊断集包含四个留出语义族的80个配对对比组，覆盖干净与15 dB噪声渲染的1,600个前缀预测。修正随机分支编码与语义标签的不匹配后，重新拟合WavLM Base Plus探针，并与匹配文本、标量声学及打乱表示探针，以及基于经过时间的基线进行比较。

Result: 重新拟合的WavLM Base Plus探针在onset后池化语义标签准确率为26.03%（95%组bootstrap区间22.14%-29.68%），在18.99%的onset前前缀上暴露动作，完整轨迹精确预测率为5.94%；其onset后标签准确率高于匹配文本、标量声学与打乱表示探针，但处于100次前缀内标签置换的第96百分位，低于97.5百分位参考值26.73%。基于经过时间的基线onset精确率更高（36.25% vs. 23.13%），但动作身份准确率更低（9.92% vs. 26.03%）。

Conclusion: 动作身份与动作时机衡量的是部分语音决策行为中两个相互独立的方面，流式口语智能体的评测应分别报告这两类指标，而非仅依赖最终轮次得分。

Abstract: Streaming spoken agents may take an external action before the available speech supports it, yet final-turn scores do not reveal whether each observed prefix supports that action. We introduce the Partial Speech Action Contract for Turn Taking in Speech Language Models (PACT-SLM), a controlled evaluation that assigns a first valid action time and measures action identity and timing separately. The primary diagnostic contains 80 paired contrast groups from four held-out semantic families and 1,600 prefix predictions across clean and 15 dB noise renderings. After correcting a mismatch between randomized branch codes and semantic labels, a refitted WavLM Base Plus probe reaches 26.03% pooled post-onset semantic-label accuracy (95% group-bootstrap interval: 22.14%-29.68%), exposes an action on 18.99% of pre-onset prefixes, and predicts 5.94% of complete trajectories exactly. It exceeds matched text, scalar-acoustic, and shuffled-representation probes in post-onset label accuracy, but its score is at the 96th percentile of 100 within-prefix label permutations and below the 97.5th-percentile reference (26.73%). Elapsed time is more onset-exact than WavLM Base Plus (36.25% vs. 23.13%) but less accurate about action identity (9.92% vs. 26.03%). These results show that action identity and timing measure distinct aspects of partial-speech decision behavior.

</details>


### [251] [Multi-Rate Bandwidth Extension by Token Completion in Neural Audio Codecs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38502)
*Benoît Ginies,Olivier Fercoq,Gaël Richard*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 本文提出将带宽扩展（BWE）重构为音频token预测问题，训练基于transformer的语言模型在解耦神经音频编解码器产生的离散表示上进行预测，并设计了一种考虑下游任务的新型编解码器，实现了高质量重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 带宽扩展是音频处理的长期难题，即从低通信号重建高频成分。近期神经架构虽有进展，但编解码器结构与下游生成任务之间缺乏有效耦合，本文旨在通过联合设计提升BWE效果。

Method: 将BWE建模为音频token预测问题；在由谐波-打击乐分解引导的解耦神经音频编解码器产生的离散表示上训练transformer语言模型；提出一种显式考虑下游token预测任务的新型编解码器设计，以更有效地耦合编解码器结构与transformer建模。

Result: 客观指标与主观评价均表明，该方法对原始信号实现了高质量重建。

Conclusion: 结果凸显了将编解码器解耦与表示学习同生成建模阶段对齐的重要性，并展示了全局、表示感知设计在推进带宽扩展方面的潜力。

Abstract: Bandwidth extension, the task of reconstructing the high-frequency components of an audio signal from its low-passed counterpart, is a long-standing problem in audio processing. In this work, we extend recent advances in neural architectures by framing bandwidth extension as an audio token prediction problem. Specifically, we train a transformer-based language model on the discrete representations produced by a disentangled neural audio codec, where the disentanglement is guided by a Harmonic-Percussive decomposition of the input signals, highlighting spectral structures particularly relevant for bandwidth extension. Our approach introduces a novel codec design that explicitly accounts for the downstream token prediction task, enabling a more effective coupling between codec structure and transformer modeling. This joint design yields high-quality reconstructions of the original signal, as measured by both objective metrics and subjective evaluations. These results highlight the importance of aligning codec disentanglement and representation learning with the generative modeling stage, and demonstrate the potential of global, representation-aware design for advancing bandwidth extension.

</details>


### [252] [RAST: Resolution-Aware Privileged Structure Transfer for Low-Resolution Audio Activity Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38780)
*Ji Hwan Park,Gautham Krishna Gudur,Yufei Shen,Dawei Liang,Edison Thomaz*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 本文提出RAST框架，通过分辨率感知的迁移学习解决高分辨率音频训练与低分辨率音频部署之间的不匹配问题，在保持推理仅用LR音频的前提下显著提升活动识别性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 音频被广泛用于人体活动识别（HAR），因其能捕捉物体交互、环境事件和上下文线索。高分辨率（HR）音频信息丰富但能耗、存储成本高，且可能泄露敏感语音内容；低分辨率（LR）音频更隐私、更高效，但采样率降低会丢失活动识别的关键声学线索，导致性能显著下降。因此需要解决这种训练-部署失配问题。

Method: 作者将该训练-部署失配形式化为"传感器分辨率特权学习"（sensor-resolution privileged learning），即训练时可用HR音频，推理时仅用LR音频。提出RAST（分辨率感知迁移框架），先通过保留token级信息和邻域结构来压缩HR教师表示，再进行局部化的HR-LR对齐。

Result: 在SAMoSA和AudioIMU数据集上，RAST持续优于仅用LR训练的基线和直接教师迁移基线，在推理仅需LR音频的情况下，将LR识别性能提升约7.8%。

Conclusion: RAST有效缓解了音频分辨率带来的训练-部署失配，证明了分辨率感知特权学习在资源受限且隐私敏感场景下的可行性，为LR音频HAR部署提供了高效方案。

Abstract: Audio is increasingly used for human activity recognition (HAR) because it captures object interactions, environmental events, and contextual cues in everyday environments. High-resolution (HR) audio provides rich acoustic information for model development but incurs substantial energy and storage costs and may expose sensitive speech content. Low-resolution (LR) audio offers a more privacy-preserving and resource-efficient alternative for deployment, but reduced sampling rates can remove acoustic cues essential for activity recognition, leading to significant performance degradation. We formulate this training-deployment mismatch as sensor-resolution privileged learning, in which HR audio is available during training, while inference relies exclusively on LR audio. We propose RAST, a resolution-aware transfer framework that compresses HR teacher representations by preserving token-level information and neighborhood structure before performing localized HR-LR alignment. Experiments on the SAMoSA and AudioIMU datasets show that RAST consistently outperforms LR-only training and direct teacher-transfer baselines, improving LR-only recognition by up to approximately 7.8% while requiring only LR audio at inference.

</details>


### [253] [Audio Token Attention Is Predictable Before the Language Model Runs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38878)
*Kyoungjun Park,Yunzhe Li,Lili Qiu*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: Triage 通过对音频 token 进行无标签线性预测的预排序剪枝，在语言模型运行前及第 2 层再次剪枝，在保持低词错率与高准确率的同时显著提升压缩率与推理吞吐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型音频语言模型（LALM）将一分钟语音编码为 750-1500 个 token，且需对每个 token 进行预填充，计算开销大。图像 token 剪枝通常在语言模型前几层之后进行，因为图像 token 在那里受关注度低；但音频 token 在那里的注意力很高且排序尚未稳定，因此需要在语言模型运行之前就对音频 token 进行排序与剪枝。

Method: 发现音频 token 在语言模型全层所受到的注意力，可从其编码器输出中线性预测。基于此，提出 Triage：用一个闭式拟合、无需标签的线性映射，在语言模型运行前预测全层注意力排序以剪枝；在多项选择题任务上，于第 2 层利用观测到的注意力再次剪枝并修正预测。压缩预算在无标签下设定，并受两个约束限制输出与全音频输出的偏差。

Result: 线性映射在 13 个 LALM 中的 11 个上以 ρ≥.69 预测全层注意力排序。保守预算下，词错率与准确率与全音频相差不超过 .04；激进预算下，在全部 12 个转写案例中优于所有基线。多项选择任务中，在 2.2-5 倍压缩下，其平均准确率比最强基线 DART 高 .043。由于在语言模型前剪枝，Qwen2.5-Omni-3B 上下文窗口可容纳的音频从 21.8 分钟提升至约 62 分钟；在最压缩配置下，单 GPU 可并发服务 4 倍的 5 分钟音频流。

Conclusion: 音频 token 的全层注意力可由编码器输出线性预测，Triage 据此实现语言模型前的预剪枝与第 2 层修正剪枝，在保持识别与理解性能的同时大幅提升压缩率、上下文容量与并发吞吐。

Abstract: A large audio language model (LALM) turns a minute of speech into 750-1,500 tokens and prefills every one. Image-token pruning often cuts after the language model's first layers, where image tokens draw little attention. Audio tokens draw much more attention there, and their ranking is still far from final, so audio needs a ranking before the language model runs. Surprisingly, the attention an audio token will receive across the language model is already linearly predictable from its encoder output, before the language model runs. A linear map, fitted in closed form without labels, predicts this all-layer attention ranking at $ρ\geq .69$ on eleven of thirteen LALMs. Our method, Triage, cuts audio tokens by this prediction and, on multiple choice, cuts again at layer 2, correcting the prediction with the attention observed there. Triage sets its compression without labels, under two budgets that limit how far its output may differ from the model's own full-audio output. At the conservative budget, its word error rate and accuracy stay within .04 of full audio. At the aggressive budget, Triage beats every baseline in all twelve transcription cases. On multiple choice, at 2.2-5x compression, it outperforms DART, the strongest baseline on average, by .043 in mean accuracy. Because it cuts before the language model, it raises the audio that fits in Qwen2.5-Omni-3B's context window from 21.8 to about 62 minutes. At its most compressive point, Triage lets one GPU serve 4x as many concurrent 5-minute streams of that model. Project page: https://audio-triage.github.io

</details>


### [254] [FFASR: Benchmarking Far-Field Automatic Speech Recognition using High-Fidelity Simulated RIRs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38897)
*Shivam Saini,Eric Bezzam,Georg Götz,Alessia Milo,Steinar Guðjónsson,Konstantinos Gkanos,Finnur Pind,Daniel Gert Nielsen*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 本文提出FFASR远场语音识别基准语料库（15,637条话语、九种条件、公开排行榜），其录音为新录制且波形不公开以防数据污染，实验显示WER从近场4.4%升至低信噪比静态条件41.3%，移动说话人带来一致的小幅性能损失，实测与仿真房间结果平均相差约1.7个百分点，支持高保真仿真作为可扩展的远场评测代理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有驱动模型选择的基准多聚焦近讲麦克风语音，而远场ASR在混响、噪声和说话人移动下性能显著退化，缺乏可控、抗数据污染的远场评测基准。

Method: 构建FFASR留出语料库：15,637条话语、来自15位说话人的新录制干语音，与14间家具房间的混合波动/几何声学RIR卷积，覆盖九种仅改变单一声学因素的条件（近场消声、实测-仿真办公室配对、高中低SNR静态远场、匹配SNR的移动说话人变体），测试波形不公开并设公开排行榜。

Result: 当代系统平均WER从近场4.4%升至静态低SNR条件的41.3%；匹配SNR下移动说话人带来小幅但一致的性能惩罚；办公室实验室配对上实测与仿真WER平均相差约1.7个百分点。

Conclusion: 在所测试条件下，高保真仿真可作为实测远场评测的可扩展代理；FFASR通过受控因素分解与防污染设计，为远场ASR鲁棒性评估提供可靠基准。

Abstract: Far-field automatic speech recognition(ASR) degrades under reverberation, noise, and talker motion, yet the benchmarks that drive model selection emphasize close-microphone speech. We present FFASR, a held-out corpus of 15,637 utterances and an open leaderboard spanning nine conditions, each varying a single acoustic factor: anechoic near-field speech, a measured-versus-simulated office-lab pair, static far-field mixtures at high/mid/low signal-to-noise ratio(SNR), and moving-talker variants at matched SNR. Dry speech from 15 talkers is convolved with hybrid wave/geometrical-acoustics room impulse responses from 14 furnished rooms; because the speech is newly recorded and the test waveforms are never released, the corpus resists training-data contamination. Across contemporary systems, mean word error rate (WER) rises from 4.4% near-field to 41.3% in the static low-SNR condition; a moving talker adds a small but consistent penalty at matched SNR; and on the office-lab pair, measured and simulated WER agree to within about 1.7 pp on average. These results support high-fidelity simulation as a scalable proxy for measured far-field evaluation under the conditions we test.

</details>


### [255] [How Reliable Are Predicted MOS for Reproducing Human System-Level Preferences in Speech Enhancement?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39032)
*Nahomi Kusunoki,Tsubasa Ochiai,Naohiro Tawara,Marc Delcroix,Naoyuki Kamo,Tetsuji Ogawa,Shoko Araki*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 本文提出系统级偏好准确率（SPA），用于衡量预测MOS与人工MOS在语音增强系统比较中是否得出一致偏好，并系统评估单模型、集成与域适应三种设定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MOS预测模型多以与人工MOS的相关性评估，但相关性高并不保证在"哪个系统更好"的系统级比较上判断一致，因此需要新的评估指标揭示这一缺口。

Method: 引入SPA指标，直接检验预测MOS与人工评分MOS是否产生相同的系统偏好；基于SPA系统评估三类设定：单一预测模型、模型集成、域适应。

Result: 单模型SPA差异显著（9.4%–76.8%）；最佳模型在约23%的系统对比中仍与人工判断不一致；集成提升有限；域适应在闭集条件下提升明显，但在目标系统与说话人均未知的开放条件下增益有限。

Conclusion: SPA能揭示基于相关性的评估无法暴露的错误，仅依赖预测MOS可能在实际语音增强系统比较中得出不可靠结论。

Abstract: We investigate whether predicted Mean Opinion Scores (MOS) can reliably support system-level comparisons of speech enhancement (SE) methods by introducing system-level preference accuracy (SPA). Although MOS prediction models are widely used to evaluate SE systems, their performance is typically assessed by correlation with human-rated MOS, which does not guarantee agreement on which system is better. SPA addresses this gap by directly evaluating whether predicted and human-rated MOS yield the same system preferences. Using SPA, we systematically evaluate three settings: single prediction models, ensembling, and domain adaptation. Through experiments, SPA varies substantially across single prediction models, from 9.4% to 76.8%. Even the best model disagrees with human judgments in approximately 23% of system comparisons. Ensembling yields only limited improvement, while domain adaptation tends to substantially improve SPA in the closed condition but brings only modest gains in the more practical open condition, where neither the target systems nor the speakers are known. These results suggest that SPA can reveal errors correlation-based evaluation alone does not expose, and that predicted MOS alone can lead to unreliable conclusions in practical SE system comparison.

</details>


### [256] [Game Sound-Effect Completion with Event-Level Transformation Hints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39044)
*Xinrui Jiang,Heng Yu*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 该论文研究游戏角色皮肤音效集的补全任务：在给定基础皮肤音频、部分目标皮肤音频及文本设计描述的条件下，生成兼具独立声学身份与原有游戏事件功能的关联音效。方法基于Stable Audio 3预训练先验，微调潜空间inpainting模型，并用带符号软保留掩码编码已有音频与可调变换提示，以控制保留与重设计的平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 新皮肤音效需要既有独特的声学身份，又要保留其在游戏事件中的功能角色，且同一套音效中不同事件所需的重新设计程度不同。现有通用音频编辑工具难以完成这种“成套、可控、关联”的音效补全。

Method: 构建了一套面向League of Legends皮肤的音效采集、处理与跨皮肤事件对齐流程；以Stable Audio 3的预训练音频先验为基础，微调潜空间inpainting模型以联合补全缺失事件；引入带符号软保留掩码（signed soft retention mask），为每个缺失事件编码可用音频与可调变换提示，显式指定保留与重设计之间的权衡。

Result: 在留出皮肤上的实验表明，该方法相较所评估的通用音频编辑器具有更好的重建效果；由目标皮肤派生的提示进一步提升了配对相似度，且三级提示即可保留连续引导的大部分收益。

Conclusion: 将皮肤音效设计形式化为条件化成套事件补全任务，并通过潜空间inpainting与可控保留—重设计掩码实现有效生成；目标派生提示与离散分级提示均能带来性能提升，为该类有条件音频生成提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Creating sound effects for a new game-character skin requires a distinct acoustic identity while preserving gameplay-event roles. The challenge is to complete a coherent set of related sounds whose required degrees of redesign differ. We formulate this task as completion conditioned on base-skin audio, completed target assets, and a textual design description. We develop a pipeline to collect, process, and align corresponding events across League of Legends skins. Building on Stable Audio 3's pretrained audio prior, we fine-tune a latent inpainting model to jointly complete missing events. A signed soft retention mask encodes available audio and an adjustable transformation hint for each missing event, specifying the requested balance between retention and redesign. Experiments on held-out skins show improved reconstruction over the evaluated general-purpose audio editors. Target-derived hints further improve paired similarity, with three-level hints retaining most of the benefit of continuous guidance.

</details>


### [257] [SCIC: Scope- and Codebook-Aware Instruction Conditioning for Speaker-Adapted Expressive TTS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39088)
*Longyu Lu,Zongwei Du,Mengtao Xing,Zhuoqun Liu,Zifan Guan,Meiguang Jin,Junfeng Ma*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 提出"说话人相对内联韵律控制"范式与SCIC方法，通过时域指令路由与码本加权提升长文本流式TTS的分句级韵律控制，并用多奖励GDPO后训练兼顾控制精度与音质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长文本实时流式TTS需要上下文相关的韵律与段落级连贯性，但现有指令式TTS多采用全局或统一条件，对分句级相对韵律变化缺乏显式控制能力。

Method: 定义说话人相对内联韵律控制：Pitch/Energy/Speed指令以同一说话人前一分句为基准做相对控制，Pause使用绝对时长区间。分析Qwen3-TTS RVQ码本发现Energy集中于早期残差码本、Pitch在更深前缀上累积，据此提出SCIC，结合帧级标签激活的Temporal Instruction Router与作用于残差码本的Tag-Specific Codebook Weighting；并采用多奖励GDPO后训练联合优化控制与质量。

Result: 相比使用文本token标签的标准指令微调，SCIC在说话人相对Pitch与Energy控制上更优；多奖励GDPO在提升控制精度的同时保持CER与说话人相似度；长文本合成中比无指令的说话人自适应SFT产生更鲜明的段落级表达层次。

Conclusion: 细粒度、码本感知的指令条件化与多奖励后训练可有效实现长文本TTS的分句级相对韵律控制，并兼顾质量与段落级表现力层次。

Abstract: Long-form live-streaming TTS requires context-dependent prosody and paragraph-level coherence. However, many existing instruction-based TTS systems use global or uniform conditions, providing limited explicit control over clause-level relative prosodic changes. We introduce Speaker-Relative Inline Prosody Control, where each Pitch, Energy, or Speed instruction targets a clause relative to the preceding clause from the same speaker, while Pause uses an absolute duration interval. In codec-based TTS, Speed and Pause affect sequence length, whereas Pitch and Energy rely on residual codebooks. By analyzing Qwen3-TTS RVQ codebooks, we find that Energy concentrates in early residual codebooks, whereas Pitch accumulates across a deeper prefix. We therefore propose Scope- and Codebook-Aware Instruction Conditioning (SCIC), combining a Temporal Instruction Router for frame-level tag activation with Tag-Specific Codebook Weighting over residual codebooks. SCIC improves speaker-relative Pitch and Energy control over standard instruction fine-tuning using text-token tags. We further apply multi-reward GDPO post-training to jointly optimize control and quality, improving control accuracy while preserving CER and speaker similarity. In long-form synthesis, SCIC produces a more distinct paragraph-level expressive hierarchy than speaker-adapted SFT without instructions. Audio demos are available at: https://taoliveaigc.github.io/SCIC/

</details>


### [258] [Training-Free Affinity Fusion of Neural and Embedding-Based Speaker Diarization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39162)
*Yehoshua Dissen,Joseph Keshet,Eduard Golshtein*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 提出免训练亲和度融合（TFAF），将神经说话人分割结构注入基于嵌入的说话人日志系统，通过构造连续亲和度矩阵并与声学亲和度融合，在AMI和CALLHOME上取得一致的DER提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于说话人嵌入的日志系统与神经日志系统利用互补的说话人信息，但二者的中间表示互不兼容，难以直接结合使用。

Method: TFAF用神经日志器推断的说话人划分对局部说话人表示进行条件化，构建连续亲和度矩阵，与基于嵌入的声学亲和度在单一全局聚类步骤前融合。无需额外训练、共享嵌入空间、说话人标签对齐，也无需硬性传递神经日志器的说话人数量。

Result: 在AMI和CALLHOME上相对两个组成系统均获得一致的DER提升；在AMI上融合还改善了说话人归属转写。消融实验表明神经说话人划分贡献了大部分增益，而保留连续的嵌入亲和度相比硬划分融合还有额外收益。

Conclusion: TFAF提供了一种无需训练、无对齐约束的框架，有效融合神经日志器与嵌入方法的互补信息，并可促进下游说话人归属转写任务。

Abstract: Speaker diarization systems based on speaker embeddings and neural diarization exploit complementary forms of speaker information, but their intermediate representations are not directly compatible. We introduce Training-Free Affinity Fusion (TFAF), which integrates the speaker structure inferred by a neural diarizer into an embedding-based diarization system. The neural speaker partition is used to condition local speaker representations, from which we construct a continuous affinity matrix and combine it with the embedding-based acoustic affinity before a single global clustering step. The method requires no additional training, shared embedding space, speaker-label alignment, or hard transfer of the neural diarizer's speaker count. Experiments on AMI and CALLHOME show consistent DER improvements over both constituent systems; on AMI, fusion also improves speaker-attributed transcription. Ablations show that the neural speaker partition accounts for most of the gain, while retaining the continuous embedding-based affinities provides additional benefit over hard partition fusion.

</details>


### [259] [UniAE-MoE: A Unified Audio Encoder via Mixture of Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39199)
*Shengbo Cai,Zhisheng Zhang,Zichao Nie,Jing Peng,Jingran Xie,Zhiyong Wu*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 提出统一音频编码器 UniAE-MoE，采用 MoE 架构融合 Qwen2-Audio 与 Audio-Flamingo 3 编码器，通过 SwiGLU 共享专家、两阶段指令微调与任务特定数据缩放（TSDS）提升跨域音频理解性能，在 XARES-LLM 基准取得 0.802 的 SOTA 成绩，并在 Interspeech 2026 音频编码器能力挑战赛中表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型音频语言模型（LALMs）的多任务性能高度依赖有效的音频编码器。现有编码器往往局限于单一音频域（语音、音乐或通用音频），难以实现跨域统一表征，因此需要一个能够融合多种主流编码器优势、具备强泛化能力的统一音频编码器。

Method: 1) 探索主流音频编码器，集成 Qwen2-Audio 与 Audio-Flamingo 3 中下游能力更优的编码器；2) 采用带共享专家的 SwiGLU 改进编码器，以解耦网络结构、促进有效模型融合；3) 引入两阶段指令微调策略，使模型更好适配多样下游任务；4) 提出任务特定数据缩放（TSDS）技术增强模型的理解能力；5) 整体基于 Mixture-of-Experts（MoE）架构构建统一音频编码器 UniAE-MoE。

Result: 在 XARES-LLM 基准上，UniAE-MoE 取得 0.802 的分数，达到 state-of-the-art 性能；在官方 Interspeech 2026 Audio Encoder Capability Challenge 中同样取得顶尖表现，展现出跨多样音频任务的稳健泛化能力。

Conclusion: 实验结果共同验证了 UniAE-MoE 在语音、音乐与通用音频领域实现统一音频理解的有效性，表明 MoE 融合架构配合共享专家 SwiGLU、两阶段指令微调及 TSDS 能够显著提升 LALMs 的跨域下游理解能力。

Abstract: Large Audio Language Models (LALMs) rely on effective audio encoders for multi-task performance. We introduce UniAE-MoE, a unified audio encoder designed to model cross-domain audio representations and achieve outstanding downstream understanding performance via a Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architecture. Specifically, we explore mainstream audio encoders and integrate those from Qwen2-Audio and Audio-Flamingo 3, which demonstrate superior downstream capabilities. To facilitate effective model fusion, we improve our encoder using SwiGLU with shared experts to decouple encoder networks, and we further introduce a two-stage instruction-tuning strategy to better adapt the model to diverse downstream tasks. Moreover, we propose the task-specific data scaling (TSDS) technique to enhance \tool's understanding capabilities. On the XARES-LLM benchmark, UniAE-MoE attains a score of 0.802, achieving state-of-the-art performance. It also delivers top-tier performance in the official Interspeech 2026 Audio Encoder Capability Challenge, further demonstrating robust generalization across diverse audio tasks. Together, these results validate the effectiveness of \tool for unified audio understanding across speech, music, and general audio domains.

</details>


### [260] [Who Said What, and Will It Be Remembered? Evaluating Persistent Speaker Attribution Across Meetings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39344)
*Shantanu Vispute,Aditya Mishra,Siddhartha Saxena*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 提出SI-cpWER指标评估持久说话人归属，ThyVoice在CHiME-8 NOTSOFAR和CHiME-6上取得最低SI-cpWER。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语音转写作为长期记忆需同时保留词内容与稳定说话人身份，但现有会议转写指标忽略说话人或在每条录音内独立重映射匿名说话人，无法衡量跨会议是否为同一人保持同一身份。

Method: 提出Speaker Identified cpWER (SI-cpWER)，在单一全局说话人ID分配下对语料整体打分；基准含五个商用"先分离后识别"级联、两个开放学术基线和ThyVoice，在CHiME-8 NOTSOFAR全部129场会议（干净与加噪）及CHiME-6上评测。ThyVoice为端到端参考系统，修复重叠并门控用于创建与更新声纹的证据。

Result: 要求持久身份后商用系统排名发生改变：ThyVoice在三种条件下SI-cpWER均低于所有评测商用级联，全榜单平均最低，47.13对次优系统54.75。辅以词法、分离、单录音归属和说话人聚类诊断，刻画最终带归属记录中的上游错误面。

Conclusion: 结果表明在跨时间复用对话的系统中必须直接评估持久说话人归属。

Abstract: Speech transcripts used as long-term memory must preserve both words and stable speaker identities. Existing meeting-transcription metrics either ignore speakers or remap anonymous speakers independently in each recording, so they cannot measure whether the same person retains one identity across meetings. We evaluate persistent speaker attribution with Speaker Identified cpWER (SI-cpWER), which scores a corpus under one global speaker-ID assignment. The benchmark covers five commercial diarize-then-identify cascades, two open academic baselines, and ThyVoice on the full 129-meeting CHiME-8 NOTSOFAR evaluation set in clean and noiseaugmented form, plus CHiME-6. ThyVoice is our end-to-end reference system; it repairs overlap and gates the evidence used to create and update voiceprints. Requiring persistent identity changes the commercial ranking: ThyVoice records lower SI-cpWER than every evaluated commercial cascade in all three conditions and the lowest mean in the full panel, 47.13 versus 54.75 for the next system. Complementary lexical, diarization, per-recording attribution, and speaker-clustering diagnostics characterize upstream error surfaces in the final attributed record. These results show why persistent attribution must be evaluated directly in systems that reuse conversations across time.

</details>


### [261] [From Speech to Editable Concepts: Probing Emotion Recognition with Concept Bottleneck Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39453)
*Hezhao Zhang,Thomas Hain*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 本文将概念瓶颈模型引入语音情感识别（SER），用转录文本、声学描述和说话人属性作为可解释概念，考察三个大语言模型在 CREMA-D、IEMOCAP、MELD 上的个体预测依赖性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 早期 SER 依赖声学特征，近期多模态（语音+文本）方法性能仍不理想；大语言模型因能联合处理多模态输入与指令而受到关注，但直接音频输入带来可解释性问题。受图像分类中概念瓶颈模型的启发，作者希望厘清 SER 预测究竟依赖哪些可解释概念。

Method: 将概念瓶颈适配到 SER，把转录文本、声学描述（如语速、强度水平）与说话人属性作为概念，由独立工具抽取；在 CREMA-D、IEMOCAP、MELD 三个数据集上对三个 LLM 进行零样本与微调实验，并通过逐一移除概念观察个体预测的变化。

Result: 在脚本化语料上，零样本 LLM 强烈偏向转录文本，CREMA-D 的 Macro-F1 由 27.8 降至 5.8；微调可消除该偏置，转录文本使 Macro-F1 由 41.8 提升至 45.1。移除语速使 CREMA-D 上 48% 的 Neutral 预测转为 Disgust；在 MELD 上移除强度水平虽使 Macro-F1 变化很小，却改变了预测结果。

Conclusion: 聚合性能指标无法反映概念移除对个体预测的影响，仅凭 Macro-F1 等总体指标会掩盖概念层面的依赖结构与预测翻转，需结合个体预测层面的分析来评估 SER 模型。

Abstract: Speech emotion recognition (SER) is the task of assigning emotion labels to utterances. Early systems relied on acoustic features, whereas recent approaches combine multiple modalities, most commonly speech and text. Still, performance remains poor on many datasets. Large language models (LLMs) have therefore attracted interest for SER, as they can process diverse inputs jointly with instructions. However, direct audio input raises questions of explainability. To address similar questions in image classification, concept bottleneck models were introduced. This work adapts concept bottlenecks to SER to examine how individual predictions depend on transcripts, acoustic descriptions and speaker attributes. Experiments test three LLMs on CREMA-D, IEMOCAP and MELD, with concepts extracted by separate tools. On scripted corpora, LLMs are strongly biased towards the transcript in the zero-shot setting, which lowers Macro-F1 from 27.8 to 5.8 on CREMA-D. Fine-tuning removes this bias, and the transcript raises Macro-F1 from 41.8 to 45.1. Removing speech rate changes 48% of Neutral predictions to Disgust on CREMA-D; removing intensity level on MELD changes predictions despite little change in Macro-F1. These findings show that aggregate performance changes alone do not capture the effects of concept removal on individual predictions.

</details>


### [262] [Ghost in the Encoder: Decodable Artist Identity Representations in Lyrics-to-Song Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39552)
*Arhan Vohra,Choenden Kyirong,Laura Ibáñez-Martínez,Martín Rocamora*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 研究表明，歌词文本本身即构成一种"艺术家级"条件通道：在ACE-Step 1.5中，仅凭歌词即可从其内部激活线性解码出艺术家身份，且该信号会在推理时从歌词编码器传播至扩散主干。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本到歌曲生成模型可被提示模仿特定艺术家或复现训练歌曲，但既有证据多为小数据集上的行为层面观察，对其背后的内部表征知之甚少；已有可解释性工作多聚焦于流派、拍号等语义概念，未涉及艺术家身份这类更具风险的属性。作者希望揭示这些现象的潜在机制，并为审计生成音乐模型"隐式学到什么"提供方法。

Method: 以ACE-Step 1.5为受控案例，构建覆盖100位艺术家、2,000首歌曲的数据集；仅使用歌词（不含任何附加标识符）作为输入，对模型内部激活进行线性探针（linear probing）解码，检验艺术家身份的可解码性，并追踪该条件信号在推理过程中从歌词编码器向扩散主干的传播路径。

Result: 模型内部激活中存在线性可解码的艺术家身份表征，仅凭歌词即可识别出对应艺术家；该条件信号会在推理阶段由歌词编码器传播至扩散主干，说明歌词被模型编码为艺术家层面的条件信息。

Conclusion: 歌词构成一条未被提示词侧防复制机制覆盖的艺术家级条件通道，提示现有基于提示过滤的防护存在盲区；同时表明潜空间分析可作为审计生成音乐模型从训练数据中隐式习得内容的有效工具。

Abstract: Text-to-song generation models can be prompted to imitate specific artists or regurgitate entire songs from their training data. Although these phenomena have been documented behaviorally on small datasets, little is known about the internal representations that may give rise to them. Prior interpretability work on generative audio has focused on locating semantic concepts such as genre or time signature within model activations. In this work, we show that a trained model can be probed for linearly decodable representations of artist identity from song lyrics alone, without any additional identifiers. Through a controlled case study of ACE-Step 1.5 spanning 2,000 songs across 100 artists, we demonstrate that the artist associated with a given set of lyrics can be identified within the model's internal activations, and that this conditioning signal propagates from the lyric encoder to the diffusion backbone during inference. These findings indicate that lyrics constitute an artist-level conditioning channel not addressed by prompt-side replication safeguards. More broadly, our work highlights how latent-space analysis can be used to audit what generative music models have implicitly learned from their training data.

</details>


### [263] [Neural Audio Codec for Robust Audio Deepfake Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39651)
*Jungwoo Kim,Joonyong Park,Junyoung Koh,Jong-Seok Lee*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 研究音频编码对深度伪造检测的影响，发现低码率与不同编解码器导致不对称的检测失败，并提出检测器引导的取证保持神经音频编解码器FP-NAC以缓解该问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有音频深度伪造检测器通常在未压缩音频上评估，而真实场景音频常经历低码率编码，导致评估与部署脱节；且编码对真实语音与伪造语音检测性能的影响机制尚不清楚。

Method: 跨编解码器、码率和检测器系统评估编码影响；提出混合配对协议以分离编码对真实与伪造音频的独立影响；进而提出FP-NAC，在保持预训练编解码器原生硬量化路径与码率的前提下，用检测器引导目标进行微调。

Result: 更低码率下检测错误更高；不同编解码器失败模式不对称：低码率DAC与EnCodec主要损害真实语音检测，X-Codec则表现出更强的伪造侧限制。在ASVspoof 2019 LA上，0.5 kbps下FP-NAC相较原始DAC将EER最多降低49.8个百分点，同时重建质量相当；仅用单一检测器监督即可提升多种检测器性能。

Conclusion: 取证透明度应作为与感知质量并列的编解码器设计目标；FP-NAC表明可通过检测器引导微调在保持码率与重建质量的同时显著提升深度伪造检测鲁棒性。

Abstract: Audio deepfake detectors are typically evaluated on uncompressed audio, although real-world audio often undergoes low-bitrate coding. In this work, we investigate how audio coding affects deepfake detection across codecs, bitrates, and detectors, finding higher errors at lower rates. A mixed-pair protocol isolates codec-induced changes in bona fide and spoof audio, revealing asymmetric, codec-dependent failures: low-rate DAC and EnCodec mainly degrade bona fide detection, whereas X-Codec shows a stronger spoof-side limitation. Motivated by these, we propose a forensic-preserving neural audio codec (FP-NAC), which fine-tunes a pretrained codec using a detector-guided objective while preserving its native hard quantization path and bitrate. On ASVspoof 2019 LA, FP-NAC reduces EER by up to 49.8~pp compared with the original DAC at 0.5~kbps while maintaining comparable reconstruction quality. Although supervised by only one detector, FP-NAC improves performance across multiple detectors, highlighting forensic transparency as a codec design objective alongside perceptual quality. Our codes are available at https://github.com/kjungwoo03/FP-NAC.

</details>


### [264] [MeanVoiceFlow2: Joint Optimization of Mean Flow and Content Encoder for Fast One-Step Zero-Shot Voice Conversion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40087)
*Takuhiro Kaneko,Hirokazu Kameoka,Kou Tanaka,Yuto Kondo*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 提出MeanVoiceFlow2，通过联合优化流匹配转换模块与高效内容编码器，并用转换蒸馏、扩散-GAN训练等策略，在零样本语音转换中实现更高感知质量与约9倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流匹配语音转换（如MeanVoiceFlow）语音质量高、说话人相似度强且支持一步推理，但其依赖计算量大的内容编码器成为效率瓶颈。

Method: 联合优化基于流匹配的转换模块与计算高效的内容编码器；采用MeanVoiceFlow的转换蒸馏与真实数据重建进行训练；引入带样本混合的扩散-GAN训练及教师引导的条件增强以提升真实感与解耦性。

Result: 零样本语音转换实验表明，MeanVoiceFlow2在保持相当说话人相似度的同时，感知质量优于MeanVoiceFlow，推理速度约提升9倍。

Conclusion: 通过联合优化转换模块与轻量内容编码器并结合蒸馏与扩散-GAN训练，可在不损失说话人相似度的前提下显著提升语音转换的效率与感知质量。

Abstract: Flow-matching approaches to voice conversion (VC) have gained attention owing to their high speech quality and strong speaker similarity. Among them, one-step models such as MeanVoiceFlow are particularly attractive because they enable efficient inference; however, their reliance on a computationally intensive content encoder remains a bottleneck. We therefore propose MeanVoiceFlow2, a framework that jointly optimizes a flow-based conversion module and a computationally efficient content encoder. The model is trained through conversion distillation using MeanVoiceFlow and the reconstruction of real data. We further incorporate diffusion-GAN training with sample mixing and teacher-guided conditioning augmentation to enhance realism and disentanglement. Experiments on zero-shot VC showed that MeanVoiceFlow2 achieved higher perceptual quality and approximately $9\times$ faster inference than MeanVoiceFlow while maintaining comparable speaker similarity. Audio samples are available at https://www.kecl.ntt.co.jp/people/kaneko.takuhiro/projects/meanvoiceflow2/.

</details>


### [265] [SE-ADD: Self-Evolving Audio Deepfake Detection with Mistake-Driven Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39679)
*Rong Wan,Wei Xie,Jiaxi Li,Wenwu Wang,Lu Yin,Yiliao Song,Xilu Wang*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 本文提出SE-ADD自进化框架，通过让音频大语言模型（ALM）基于自身判错进行LoRA迭代微调，从而在持续演变的伪造攻击环境下提升音频深伪检测的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于ALM的音频深伪检测依赖预定义的监督信号（真实标签或已验证的法证依据），忽略了模型自身错误所指示的、最需要针对性监督的位置。同时，部署后新攻击会不断涌现并成为主导，旧攻击仍持续存在，模型需要在这种演变环境中持续适应。

Method: 1）引入"演变伪造环境"，即新攻击成为主导而旧攻击持续存在；2）提出SE-ADD自进化框架，利用模型自身判决与自生成法证线索构建"错误驱动监督"；3）通过LoRA对ALM进行迭代适配，所有训练样本接受真实性直接监督，误分类样本额外接受线索增强监督；4）随着ALM更新，判决与线索被重新生成，监督信号随之进化。

Result: 在两个ALM上的实验表明，SE-ADD在泛化到未见攻击方面有效：Qwen2-Audio的等错误率（EER）从36.72%降至7.52%，MOSS-Audio从19.93%降至3.97%。

Conclusion: 从"从错误中学习"的视角出发，利用模型自身错误构建动态演化的监督信号，可显著提升ALM音频深伪检测在持续演变攻击环境下的泛化性能。

Abstract: Audio deepfake detection (ADD) must remain effective when new spoofing attacks emerge after deployment. Emerging audio language model (ALM)-based ADD methods are built on predefined supervision from ground-truth labels or verified forensic rationales. However, this paradigm overlooks an ALM's own mistakes, which indicate where targeted supervision is most needed. To this end, we first introduce evolving spoofing environments for ALM-based ADD, where a new attack becomes dominant while previously observed attacks persist. Motivated by the above learning-from-mistakes perspective, we further propose SE-ADD, a self-evolving framework that iteratively adapts an ALM via low-rank adaptation (LoRA) using mistake-driven supervision built from its verdicts and self-generated forensic cues. All training samples receive direct authenticity supervision, while misclassified ones receive additional cue-augmented supervision. As verdicts and cues are regenerated by the updated ALM, the resulting supervision evolves accordingly. Experiments on two ALMs demonstrate the effectiveness of SE-ADD in generalizing to unseen attacks, reducing the equal error rate (EER) from $36.72\%$ to $7.52\%$ for Qwen2-Audio and from $19.93\%$ to $3.97\%$ for MOSS-Audio.

</details>


### [266] [Synthetic Speech Attribution via Prototypical Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39722)
*Viola Negroni,Paolo Bestagini,Stefano Tubaro*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 本文提出将原型网络(PProtoPNet)应用于合成语音归因任务,基于频谱图表示,在MLAAD数据集上验证原型推理能在保持竞争力的同时提供基于样例的可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有合成语音归因方法多依赖黑盒神经网络,决策过程缺乏可解释性,难以说明预测依据。

Method: 将ProtoPNet适配到基于频谱图的语音表示,通过预测与代表性训练样例的比较实现归因;在MLAAD数据集上评估闭集、跨语言与开集三种设定。

Result: 原型推理在归因性能上与基线相当或更优,同时能够提供基于样例的解释。

Conclusion: 在合成语音归因中,通过原型建模可以同时实现可解释性与高性能。

Abstract: Synthetic speech attribution aims to identify the generative system responsible for a speech signal, but current approaches typically rely on black-box neural networks that provide limited insight into their decisions. This work investigates prototype-based networks as an interpretable alternative, where predictions are grounded in comparisons with representative training examples. We adapt ProtoPNet to spectrogram-based speech representations and evaluate the proposed framework on the MLAAD dataset under closed-set, cross-lingual, and open-set conditions. Experiments show that prototype-based reasoning achieves competitive or improved attribution performance compared with the baseline while enabling example-based explanations. These results highlight that interpretability and performance can be jointly achieved in synthetic speech attribution through prototype-based modeling.

</details>


### [267] [SEAR: Spoofing Evidence-Grounded Audio Reasoning Benchmark for Audio Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39847)
*Rong Wan,Suliu Qin,Jiaxi Li,Wei Xie,Wenwu Wang,Xiaolong Han,Lu Yin,Xilu Wang*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 该论文针对音频深度伪造检测基准缺乏声学证据验证的问题，提出SEAR四任务基准与BAEA声学证据智能体，以证据落地的推理方式评估并改进音频语言模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有音频语言模型（ALM）用于音频深度伪造检测（ADD）的基准，仅评估其判定结论或推理理由的表面合理性，未验证支撑理由的底层声学证据，导致"理由看似合理"与"证据可验证"之间存在脱节。

Method: 1) 提出SEAR（spoofing evidence-grounded audio reasoning），一个四任务音频问答基准，从声学证据识别、证据量化、深度伪造检测、取证理由生成四个维度评估基于ALM的ADD；2) 提出BAEA（bona-fide-based acoustic evidence agent），在冻结的ALM上接入受控声学工具，并采用Fixed（固定）与Adaptive（自适应）两种证据获取策略；3) 对六种ALM进行实验，并通过受控干预（注入误导性证据）考察检测与证据落地性能的变化。

Result: 六种ALM的实验显示，合理的推理理由与可验证的声学证据推理之间存在明显差距；BAEA-Fixed在两个评估分区上均提升了最终判定与取证理由的质量；受控干预表明误导性证据会同时降低检测性能与证据落地性能。

Conclusion: 仅凭理由的表面合理性不足以评估ALM的音频伪造检测能力，必须以可验证的声学证据为落脚点；引入受控声学工具与证据获取策略的BAEA可有效提升检测结论与取证推理的可信度，同时揭示模型对误导性证据的脆弱性。

Abstract: Audio language models (ALMs) are increasingly used for audio deepfake detection (ADD), yet existing benchmarks assess their verdicts or rationale plausibility without verifying the underlying acoustic evidence. To address this issue, we first introduce spoofing evidence-grounded audio reasoning (SEAR), a four-task AQA benchmark to evaluate ALM-based ADD through acoustic evidence identification and quantification, deepfake detection, and forensic rationale generation. We further propose a bona-fide-based acoustic evidence agent (BAEA), which equips a frozen ALM with controlled acoustic tools under \textsc{fixed} or \textsc{adaptive} evidence-acquisition policies. Experiments with six ALMs reveal a clear gap between plausible rationales and verifiable acoustic evidence reasoning, while BAEA-\textsc{Fixed} improves final verdicts and forensic rationales on both evaluation partitions. Controlled interventions further show that misleading evidence degrades both detection and grounding performance.

</details>


<div id='eess.AS'></div>

# eess.AS [[Back]](#toc)

### [268] [Monotonicity-Guided Semantic Alignment for Zero-shot Multispeaker Image-to-Speech Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38440)
*Lijun Wang,Yixian Lu,Shogo Okada*

Main category: eess.AS

TL;DR: MGSA是首个零样本多说话人直接图像到语音合成框架，利用语义语音单元与软单调对齐先验，实现视觉内容到有序语音序列的映射。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图像到语音（Img2Sp）面临对齐挑战：图像允许多种口头描述，与语音序列之间缺乏单调对应关系；同时现有方法多局限于单说话人，难以支持未见说话人的零样本合成。

Method: 提出单调性引导语义对齐（MGSA）框架：采用语义语音单元作为跨说话人的共享内容目标，并用参考语音进行说话人条件建模；查询对齐器通过软单调先验将视觉内容中学到的语义记忆映射到语音单元位置，生成位置特定的条件状态；再使用分块掩码扩散生成器在这些状态下生成语音单元。

Result: 在Flickr8k-Audio上，字幕生成性能与单说话人基线具有竞争力；使用LibriTTS-R参考语音的评估支持对未见说话人的零样本合成；消融实验验证了对齐器与分块扩散的有效性。

Conclusion: MGSA通过语义单元共享目标、软单调对齐与分块掩码扩散，有效解决了图像到语音的非单调对齐问题，并实现了零样本多说话人语音合成。

Abstract: Direct image-to-speech (Img2Sp) poses an alignment challenge in mapping visual content to ordered speech sequences, as images permit multiple spoken descriptions and lack monotonic correspondence with speech sequences. We propose Monotonicity-Guided Semantic Alignment (MGSA), to the best of our knowledge, the first framework for zero-shot multispeaker Img2Sp synthesis. We use semantic speech units to provide shared content targets across speakers with reference speech for speaker conditioning. A query aligner maps semantic memory learned from visual content to speech unit positions via a soft monotonic prior, which yields position-specific conditioning states. A blockwise masked diffusion generator is employed for the speech unit generation conditioning on these states. Experiments on Flickr8k-Audio show competitive captioning performance against single-speaker baselines, while evaluation with LibriTTS-R references supports zero-shot synthesis for unseen speakers. Ablations validate the effectiveness of aligner and block diffusion. Audio samples are available at https://alizeded.github.io/mgsa-demo.

</details>


### [269] [Voices as Handles: Reasoning about Speaker Identity with Frozen Text LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38501)
*Runqiu Xu,Zhisheng Zheng,David Harwath*

Main category: eess.AS

TL;DR: 提出Speaker Handles（软令牌）将声学说话人身份注入冻结文本LLM，并构建SpeakerBind基准，实现跨会话说话人相关推理，在VoxCeleb1达97.40–98.36%、SpeakerBind达70.40%（接近71.88%上限）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多用户语音代理需跨会话追踪"谁说了什么"。文本LLM是理想骨干，但转录文本不含声学说话人身份，模型缺少跨会话将信息关联到具体说话人的持久参照，该空白亟待填补。

Method: 引入Speaker Handles——向冻结文本LLM暴露声学说话人身份的软令牌表示；采用三阶段课程训练轻量投影器（参数量不足骨干的0.1%），将说话人嵌入映射为这些handles。同时构建SpeakerBind受控共享代理基准：用户间事实重叠，必须依赖正确的跨会话说话人归属才能作答，避免文本线索泄漏事实归属。

Result: Speaker Handles在VoxCeleb1上准确率97.40–98.36%，在SpeakerBind上达70.40%，接近71.88%的to p l i n e上限。

Conclusion: 结果表明Speaker Handles为冻结文本LLM整合声学说话人身份、支撑说话人—内容推理提供了一种高效途径。

Abstract: Multi-user voice agents must track who said what across dialogue sessions. Text LLMs are attractive backbones for such agents, but transcripts alone do not expose acoustic speaker identity, leaving the model without a persistent reference for linking information to speakers across sessions. We address this gap by introducing Speaker Handles, soft-token representations that expose acoustic speaker identity to a frozen text LLM for cross-session speaker-dependent reasoning. A three-stage curriculum trains a lightweight projector, with fewer than 0.1% of the backbone's parameters, to map speaker embeddings into these handles. Establishing whether the resulting handles truly support cross-session speaker-dependent reasoning is challenging with existing benchmarks because textual cues can partially reveal fact ownership. We therefore present SpeakerBind, a controlled shared-agent benchmark in which overlapping facts across users require correct cross-session speaker attribution. Speaker Handles achieve 97.40-98.36% accuracy on VoxCeleb1 and 70.40% on SpeakerBind, close to the 71.88% topline. These results show that the proposed Speaker Handles provide an efficient way to integrate acoustic speaker identity into frozen text LLMs for speaker-content reasoning.

</details>


### [270] [Tacit-TTS: From Autoregressive Decoding to Masked Prediction for Efficient Transcript-Free Voice Cloning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38658)
*Jian Chen,You Zhang,Mark Vinton*

Main category: eess.AS

TL;DR: Tacit-TTS 是一种高效的无转写零样本语音克隆系统，通过蒸馏 IndexTTS2 实现非自回归生成，在保持竞争性零样本质量的同时，生成速度比 IndexTTS2 快 10 倍以上，并支持跨语言与非词汇参考。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归语义建模的 TTS 系统虽在零样本语音克隆与表现力上表现优异，但顺序解码导致高延迟；非自回归方案虽快，却依赖更受限的参考条件（如推理时需参考语音的转写文本）。因此需要一种既高效又无需转写的零样本克隆方案。

Method: 提出 Tacit-TTS，从 IndexTTS2 蒸馏得到：(1) 用掩码非自回归生成替代自回归文本到语义解码；(2) 引入免训练声学长度的估计；(3) 通过 ReFlow 蒸馏加速流匹配渲染器。

Result: 在两个英语和两个普通话数据集上，Tacit-TTS 达到有竞争力的零样本质量，超过 5 秒的语音生成速度比 IndexTTS2 快 10 倍以上；其无转写条件还支持跨语言和非词汇参考（八种其他语言、婴儿咿呀声、合成乱语），而依赖转写的系统常因 ASR 转写不可靠而退化或失败。

Conclusion: Tacit-TTS 通过非自回归蒸馏实现了高效、免转写的零样本语音克隆，在速度与质量之间取得良好平衡，并拓展了参考条件的使用场景。

Abstract: TTS systems with autoregressive semantic modeling have demonstrated strong zero-shot voice cloning performance and rich expressive variation, but their sequential decoding incurs substantial latency. Non-autoregressive alternatives offer much faster generation, yet often rely on more restrictive reference conditioning, such as requiring transcripts of the reference speech during inference. We present Tacit-TTS, an efficient transcript-free zero-shot voice cloning system distilled from IndexTTS2. Our model replaces autoregressive text-to-semantic decoding with masked non-autoregressive generation, introduces training-free acoustic length estimation, and accelerates the flow-matching renderer through ReFlow distillation. Across two English and two Mandarin datasets, Tacit-TTS achieves competitive zero-shot quality while generating speech over 10x faster than IndexTTS2 for utterances longer than 5 seconds. Its transcript-free conditioning further supports cross-lingual and non-lexical references. We validate this capability using references from eight other languages, infant babble, and synthetic gibberish, where transcript-dependent systems often degrade or fail due to unreliable ASR transcripts.

</details>


### [271] [A barrier or a booster? Familiarity effects on Mandarin emotion prosody recognition using AI-powered voice cloning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38794)
*Feng Xu,Gaoyuan Zhang,Shanshan Xue,Yixiang Chen,Hanrui Zhou,Xurong Xie,Hui Chen*

Main category: eess.AS

TL;DR: 本研究探讨语音来源（人类 vs. AI合成）与说话人熟悉度如何影响情绪韵律识别，采用行为与生理指标进行被试内实验，发现人类语音的识别准确率更高、反应更快，而HRV未显示显著差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 情绪韵律感知需同时处理声学线索与说话人身份。自然语音解码对听者而言较为轻松，但AI合成语音存在细微的声学异常，带来认知复杂性。目前尚不清楚这些合成语音特征如何与听者先前的社会知识及对熟悉说话人的记忆相互作用。

Method: 采用被试内设计，以普通话母语成人为被试，完成情绪识别任务，同时采集行为数据（准确率、反应时）与生理数据（心率变异性，HRV）。自变量为语音来源（人类 vs. AI）与说话人熟悉度。

Result: 人类语音条件下识别准确率显著高于AI语音，且加工速度更快；HRV在各条件间无显著差异。

Conclusion: 合成语音的解码受自上而下的社会认知调控，揭示了当前AI语音合成技术的局限。

Abstract: Emotion prosody perception requires simultaneous processing of acoustic cues and speaker identity. While listeners effortlessly decode natural speech, AI synthetic voices introduce cognitive complexities due to subtle acoustic atypicalities. It remains unclear how these synthetic features interact with a listener's prior social knowledge and memory of a familiar speaker. This study investigated how speech sources (human vs. AI) and speaker familiarity affect emotion recognition accuracy and cognitive load. A within-subject task with Mandarin-speaking adults evaluated behavioral (accuracy, reaction time) and physiological data (heart rate variability). Results showed that human voices yielded significantly higher accuracy and faster processing times than AI voices, while HRV did not significantly differentiate between conditions. These findings show that decoding synthetic speech is gated by top-down social cognition, highlighting limitations in current AI synthesis technologies.

</details>


### [272] [VOSSA: Voiceprint Optimization for Streaming Speech Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38887)
*Mu-Ruei Tseng,Waris Quamer,Ghady Nasrallah,Ricardo Gutierrez-Osuna*

Main category: eess.AS

TL;DR: 提出 VOSSA，一种用于流式语音转换的说话人表征框架，从内容编码器中间层提取说话人信息并用注意力统计池化聚合，与 VC 目标联合训练，无需独立说话人编码器，在多项数据集上提升 F0 动态与元音区分度且保持其他指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实时语音转换通常依赖 ASV 预训练说话人嵌入，但这类嵌入训练目标是说话人内部跨音段与韵律变化保持稳定，这与流式约束下的帧级声学生成存在冲突。

Method: VOSSA 从内容编码器的中间层提取说话人信息，使用注意力统计池化进行聚合，并与语音转换目标联合训练，从而移除对独立说话人编码器的需求。

Result: 在六个数据集上，VOSSA 改善了 F0 动态与具有元音区分性的声学线索，同时保持 NISQA-MOS、WER 与说话人相似度相当；感知测试进一步显示自然度、说话人相似度、可懂度和生动性均有提升。

Conclusion: 该框架通过联合训练的内容编码器中间层表征替代独立 ASV 说话人嵌入，缓解了稳定性训练目标与流式帧级生成之间的冲突，在客观与主观指标上均取得收益。

Abstract: Real-time voice conversion (VC) systems commonly rely on pretrained speaker embeddings from automatic speaker verification (ASV) models. While effective for speaker discrimination, these embeddings are trained to remain stable across phonetic and prosodic variations within-speaker, which may conflict with frame-level acoustic generation in streaming constraints. To address this issue, we propose VOSSA (Voiceprint Optimization for Streaming Speech Architectures), a speaker representation framework that extracts speaker information from intermediate content encoder layers and aggregates using attentive statistics pooling. The embedding is trained jointly with VC objectives, removing the need for a separate speaker encoder. Across six datasets, VOSSA improves F0 dynamics and vowel-discriminative acoustic cues while maintaining comparable NISQA-MOS, WER, and speaker similarity. Perceptual tests further indicate improvements in naturalness, speaker similarity, intelligibility, and vibrancy.

</details>


### [273] [Improving Predicted MOS Scores, Not Perceived Quality: Multi-Predictor Test-Time Optimization of Enhanced Speech](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39028)
*Tsubasa Ochiai,Marc Delcroix,Nahomi Kusunoki,Rintaro Ikeshita,Naohiro Tawara,Naoyuki Kamo,Tetsuji Ogawa,Shoko Araki*

Main category: eess.AS

TL;DR: 该论文首次系统分析语音增强任务中针对MOS预测器的测试时优化，发现优化仅提升被优化的预测分数，参考指标与人类感知质量均无改善，提示优化用预测器不应被用于评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非侵入式MOS预测器被广泛用于替代主观听测来评估和排序语音增强系统，其假设是分数越高则语音质量越好。若该假设成立，则提升预测分数应带来更高质量的语音。作者由此检验这一假设是否成立，尤其关注对增强信号进行测试时优化以抬高MOS分数的做法是否会误导评估。

Method: 作者对语音增强任务进行了首次全面的测试时优化分析：直接修改增强后的信号，使多个MOS预测器分数的平均值提高（即test-time optimization）。在URGENT 2026挑战赛的七个系统上开展实验，对比优化前后被优化预测分数、未参与优化的预测分数、参考型指标以及MUSHRA主观听测结果的变化。

Result: 实验发现三点：1) 所有被优化的预测分数均上升，而参考型指标几乎不变；2) 未参与优化的MOS预测分数不上升；3) MUSHRA主观听测显示感知质量没有改善。

Conclusion: 这些结果表明此类优化可能扭曲评估，例如在忽略实际感知质量的情况下使系统间比较产生偏差。作者建议：用于优化的预测器不应用于评估，且挑战赛应保持用于排序的预测器不公开。

Abstract: Non-intrusive MOS predictors are widely used instead of subjective listening tests to evaluate and rank speech enhancement (SE) systems. If they accurately reflect perceived quality, raising their scores should lead to higher-quality speech. We present the first comprehensive analysis of test-time optimization for the SE task, which directly modifies the enhanced signal to raise the average of multiple MOS predictor scores. On seven systems from the URGENT 2026 challenge, we find that 1)~all the optimized predicted scores increase while reference-based metrics remain nearly unchanged, 2)~a non-optimized predicted score does not increase, and 3)~a MUSHRA listening test shows no improvement in perceived quality. These findings reveal a risk that such optimization can distort evaluations, e.g., biasing comparisons of SE systems regardless of their perceived quality.
