We believe these findings can inform future evaluation practices: they suggest that predictors used for optimization should not be used for evaluation, and that challenges should keep the predictors used for ranking undisclosed.

</details>


### [274] [SURE-EVAL: A Systematic and Unified Agentic Framework for Reproducible Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39030)
*Jing Peng,Junhao Du,Yixuan Wang,Bowen Wang,Hanqi Li,Chaolei Liu,Weihan Chen,Haohui Xie,Ruichen Sun,Chenghao Wang,Wen Wen,Guanyu Chen,Xiaoyu Gu,Haoyu Li,Yiwei Guo,Bohan Li,Tao Liu,Yucheng Wang,Yu Xi,Yihua Zhou,Qiang Zhou,Feng Lu,Shuai Wang,Kai Yu*

Main category: eess.AS

TL;DR: 提出 SURE-EVAL——一个系统性、统一的智能体（Agentic）框架，用于音频与语音系统的可复现评测，将异构模型接入、受控推理与版本化打分统一到一个可执行工作流中，并在 18 个公开模型上验证其可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 音频与语音模型发布迅速，但报告分数常将"模型能力"与"部署及评测选择"混为一谈：同一 checkpoint 在不同运行时、硬件、解码设置或回退策略下会产生不同预测；即便预测固定，不同归一化方式与指标实现也会给出不同分数。现有语音基准多只标准化部分数据集或打分流程，鲜少把异构模型接入、受控推理与版本化打分串联为单一可执行工作流。

Method: 框架包含两个核心工作流：(1) Tool Agent Workflow——将模型发布转换为隔离的、经过验证的可调用工具（isolated, verified callable tools）；(2) Main Agent Workflow——先固定任务特定的推理与打分协议，再把所有"影响分数"的操作委派给版本化的确定性程序（versioned deterministic programs）。每条结果均保存其运行时、协议、流水线节点、预测结果与审计工件（audit artifacts）。评测覆盖 ASR、TTS、语音转换、说话人日志、说话人属性识别及多任务音频理解。

Result: 在 18 个公开模型发布上，纯 Codex baseline 一次性仅完成 12 个模型，而加入 SURE-EVAL Tool Agent Workflow 的同一智能体完成全部 18 个。另开展覆盖 7 个 ASR 测试条件与 2 个 TTS 子集的统一评测；对三套 TTS 系统的协议分析发现，论文报告分数与统一评测结果存在 0.02–0.52 的绝对分差，且差异方向随模型和语言而变化。

Conclusion: 可复现评测必须同时控制"模型执行"与"输出打分"两个环节；仅标准化数据或仅标准化指标都不足以消除评测偏差，而 SURE-EVAL 通过 Agentic 工作流 + 版本化确定性打分实现了端到端的可控与可审计。

Abstract: Audio and speech models are released rapidly, but reported scores often conflate model capability with deployment and evaluation choices. The same checkpoint can produce different predictions under different runtimes, hardware, decoding settings, or fallback policies. Even fixed predictions can receive different scores under different normalization and metric implementations. Existing speech benchmarks standardize selected datasets or scoring procedures, but rarely connect heterogeneous model onboarding, controlled inference, and versioned scoring in one executable workflow. We introduce SURE-EVAL, a Systematic and Unified Agentic framework for Reproducible Evaluation of audio and speech systems. A Tool Agent Workflow converts model releases into isolated, verified callable tools. A Main Agent Workflow commits task-specific inference and scoring protocols, then delegates all score-bearing operations to versioned deterministic programs. Each result retains its runtime, protocol, pipeline nodes, predictions, and audit artifacts. Across 18 public releases covering automatic speech recognition, text-to-speech, voice conversion, speaker diarization, speaker-attributed recognition, and multi-task audio understanding, a Codex-only baseline completes 12 models in one shot, while the same agent with the SURE-EVAL Tool Agent Workflow completes all 18. We also conduct unified evaluations over seven ASR test conditions and two TTS subsets. A protocol analysis of three TTS systems finds absolute differences of 0.02-0.52 points between paper-reported and unified results, with the direction varying by model and language. These results show that reproducible evaluation requires controlling both model execution and output scoring.

</details>


### [275] [PG-SELD: Physics-Guided Sound Event Localization and Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39216)
*Elad Cohen,Elad Dror Cohen,Arnon Netzer,Hai Victor Habi*

Main category: eess.AS

TL;DR: 本文提出 PG-SELD 训练框架，利用自由场物理模型作为房间无关参考，通过物理引导知识蒸馏对齐混响信号与自由场教师网络的中间表征，从而提升 SELD 模型的跨房间泛化能力，在 STARSS23 上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SELD（声事件定位与检测）需从多通道音频中联合识别声事件并估计到达方向。尽管深度学习方法性能强劲，但房间混响会给学习到的表征注入环境相关特征，导致模型跨声学环境泛化能力受限。作者因此希望找到一种与房间无关的参考信号，以缓解环境特定信息的干扰。

Method: 提出 PG-SELD 训练框架，将自由场（free-field）模型与物理引导知识蒸馏相结合。具体而言，以自由场模型作为教师，对匹配的声学场景，强制学生模型从混响信号中提取的中间表征与教师从自由场信号中产生的表征对齐。该引导促使模型保留与声事件和定位相关的信息，同时降低对房间特定特性的敏感性。

Result: 在 STARSS23 基准上，PG-SELD 对多种基线 SELD 架构均能持续提升其泛化性能。

Conclusion: 以自由场物理模型作为房间无关的参考并结合知识蒸馏，是一种提升 SELD 模型跨房间泛化能力的有效且通用的训练策略，可即插即用地增强现有基线架构。

Abstract: Sound event localization and detection (SELD) aims to jointly recognize sound events and estimate their directions of arrival from multichannel audio. Although recent deep learning approaches have achieved strong performance, their ability to generalize across acoustic environments remains limited, as room reverberation introduces environment-specific characteristics into the learned representations. In this work, we address this challenge by leveraging a physical free-field model as a room-independent reference. Specifically, we propose PG-SELD, a training framework that combines free-field with physics-guided knowledge distillation. Our approach aligns intermediate representations extracted from reverberant signals with those produced by a free-field teacher for matched acoustic scenes. This guidance encourages the model to preserve event- and localization-relevant information while reducing sensitivity to room-specific characteristics. Experimental results on the STARSS23 benchmark show that PG-SELD consistently improves the generalization performance of multiple baseline SELD architectures.

</details>


### [276] [DuSpaR: Dual-State Sparsifying Recurrent Unit with Feedback Modulation for Compute-Efficient Speech Processing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39237)
*Zixiao Li,Sheng Zhou,Longbiao Cheng,Shih-Chii Liu*

Main category: eess.AS

TL;DR: 本文提出DuSpaR——一种面向边缘设备语音处理的高效循环单元，通过双状态循环与ReLU稀疏化矩阵-向量乘输入，动态跳过零元素以节省计算与权重访存，在KWS、SLU、SE三类任务上以更低计算量取得更优或相当性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 资源受限边缘设备上的语音处理模型需要兼顾计算效率与性能。现有循环模型（如GRU）计算开销较大，且稀疏感知循环模型的效率与精度平衡仍有提升空间，因此需要一种更高效的循环构建模块。

Method: 提出双状态稀疏化循环单元（DuSpaR）：采用双状态循环在带状态的反馈环路中调制输入向量；其循环单元利用ReLU激活对参与矩阵-向量乘的输入向量操作数进行稀疏化；推理时动态跳过零元素，从而减少乘累加操作与权重内存读取。

Result: 在相似参数量下，KWS与SLU上比GRU分别减少51.0%和68.4%计算量且精度更高；SE上减少50.1%计算量并保持相近质量。在可比计算成本及多种模型规模下，KWS/SLU精度与SE质量均优于其他稀疏感知循环模型。消融显示，相比单状态循环基线，双状态循环在任务性能相近时将有效计算量降低3.1至11.3倍。

Conclusion: DuSpaR通过双状态循环与输入稀疏化有效降低推理计算与访存开销，在多种语音任务上实现优于GRU及稀疏感知基线模型的效率-性能权衡，适合边缘设备部署。

Abstract: We introduce the Dual-state Sparsifying Recurrent Unit (DuSpaR) as a computationally efficient building block for speech processing models on resource-constrained edge devices. It employs dual-state recurrence to modulate its input vectors in a stateful feedback loop. Its recurrent cells sparsify the input vector operand involved in matrix-vector multiplication using ReLU activation. By skipping the zero entries dynamically, inference-time savings in multiply-accumulate operations and weight memory fetches can be achieved. We evaluate DuSpaR on three speech tasks: keyword spotting (KWS) on the Google Speech Commands dataset, spoken language understanding (SLU) on the Fluent Speech Commands dataset, and speech enhancement (SE) on the Voice Bank + Demand (VBD) dataset. At similar parameter counts, DuSpaR requires 51.0% and 68.4% less computation than Gated Recurrent Unit (GRU) on KWS and SLU, respectively, while achieving higher accuracy, and 50.1% less computation on SE while maintaining similar quality. At comparable computational cost and across a range of model sizes, DuSpaR also achieves higher KWS/SLU accuracy and better SE quality than other sparsity-aware recurrent models. Ablation studies show that compared with the single-state recurrence baseline, dual-state recurrence reduces the effective compute by factors of 3.1 to 11.3 at similar task performance.

</details>


### [277] [Bin2Ambi: Learning Ambisonic Soundfield Reconstruction from Head-Tracked Binaural Audio](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39732)
*Gavin Milner,Nils Peters*

Main category: eess.AS

TL;DR: 提出并定义新任务Bin2Ambi（双耳到Ambisonics转换），利用智能耳机运动传感器同步采集的头部追踪数据，解决双耳音频方向不确定性问题，实现空间音频转换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 用户生成内容成为主流消费内容，但消费级硬件难以采集空间音频。智能耳机普及使双耳录音成为可行方案，然而双耳信号的固有特性（前后定位模糊、混淆锥侧向误差）限制了其作为录音格式的可用性。

Method: 提出并定义Bin2Ambi任务；系统利用智能耳机运动传感器同步采集的头部追踪数据，辅助解决双耳音频的固有方向不确定性，学习方向性和扩散场信息进行Ambisonics声场转换。

Result: 转换后的Ambisonics声场平均方向误差低至11.8°（up to 11.8°），感知空间质量与DirAC基准模型相当；头部追踪数据显著减少极端定位误差。

Conclusion: 所提算法可作为未来Bin2Ambi新任务改进的基线。

Abstract: User-generated content has become one of the most-consumed content types. However, capturing spatial audio with consumer hardware is still challenging. Given the widespread success of smart earbuds, binaural audio could be a promising option to capture spatial audio on consumer devices, but its inherent signal characteristic limits its usability as a recording format. In this paper, we propose and define a new task, Binaural to Ambisonics conversion (Bin2Ambi). In our proposed system, we exploit simultaneously captured head-tracking data provided from the motion sensors in smart earbuds. We show that this motion data help resolve the inherent directional uncertainty of two-channel binaural audio due to front-back localization ambiguities and lateral errors in the cone of confusion. Our results show that our system learns directional and diffuse-field information and that head-tracking especially reduces extreme localization errors. Objective metrics and a subjective listening test suggest that the converted Ambisonics soundfield achieves an average directional error of up to $11.8^\circ$ and a perceived spatial quality similar to a DirAC ground-truth model. The proposed algorithm can serve as a baseline for future improvements to this novel Bin2Ambi task.

</details>


### [278] [Pitch Smoothing Using Relative Interval Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39852)
*Chin-Yun Yu,Chi-Jen Peng,Li Su,György Fazekas*

Main category: eess.AS

TL;DR: 提出相对音程网络（RIN），一种轨迹平滑框架，用数据驱动的多跳音高差来校正逐帧基频估计，将平滑建模为L1范数优化并证明等价于最小费用流问题，用线性规划高效求解，在语音、歌唱与器乐数据集上优于或匹配Viterbi解码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统Viterbi平滑器只强制一阶连续性，缺乏长时时间感知能力，在被污染的帧上容易锁定八度误差。

Method: 用Variable-Q变换互相关提取任意帧偏移下的鲁棒相对音高音程；将音高平滑表述为L1范数优化问题，并证明其等价于最小费用流问题，通过线性规划高效求解。

Result: 在语音、歌唱与器乐数据集上，RIN显著改善弱估计器，在相近计算成本下匹配或超越Viterbi解码，并在特定声学退化条件下具有更强的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: RIN通过引入数据驱动的多跳相对音程约束，弥补了常规Viterbi平滑器缺乏长时感知与易受八度误差影响的缺陷，为音高轨迹平滑提供了有效且计算可行的替代方案。

Abstract: Pitch tracking systems typically couple a per-frame fundamental frequency ($F_0$) estimator with a temporal smoothing stage to obtain continuous trajectories. Conventional Viterbi smoothers enforce first-order continuity but lack long-term temporal awareness and could lock into octave errors across corrupted frames. We propose Relative Interval Networks (RIN), a trajectory smoothing framework that reconciles per-frame pitch estimates with data-driven multi-hop pitch differences. We extract robust relative pitch intervals across arbitrary frame offsets using Variable-Q Transform cross-correlation. We formulate pitch smoothing as an $L_1$-norm optimization problem and prove its equivalence to a minimum cost circulation problem, solved efficiently via linear programming. Evaluations across speech, singing, and instrumental datasets show that RIN substantially improves weak estimators, matches or outperforms Viterbi decoding at a comparable computational cost, and provides superior robustness under certain acoustic degradation.

</details>
